题录总数：72808；含摘要：0；目录已提取：0；低置信分类：10862。

分类同时使用题名、关键词、摘要和目录标题。三级专题由四级受控词汇触发，每个词条均保存对应论文 ID；论文可同时归入多个目的、系统和专题。

## 四级：详细词汇与对应论文

### 早期胚胎与形态发生

- 卵裂：56 篇；Hypochlorite detection via the irreversible imine-bond cleavage by a practical fluorescent sensor: applications to smartphone-assisted environmental water and food bioimaging / 通过实用荧光传感器实现不可逆的亚胺键解理检测次氯酸盐：在智能手机辅助环境水和食品生物成像中的应用；Metal-Catalyzed Abiotic Cleavage of C═C Bonds for Effective Fluorescence Imaging of Cu(II) and Fe(III) in Living Systems. / 金属催化的C═C键非生物断裂，用于活性系统中Cu（II）和Fe（III）的有效荧光成像。；Protease-mediated activation of Par2 elicits calcium waves during zebrafish egg activation and blastomere cleavage. / 蛋白酶介导的Par2活化在斑马鱼卵活化和爆裂体裂解过程中引发钙波。；Septins promote macrophage pyroptosis by regulating gasdermin D cleavage and ninjurin-1-mediated plasma membrane rupture. / 隔蛋白通过调节gasdermin D的切割和ninjurin-1介导的质膜破裂，促进巨噬细胞的热剥作用。；Oxidative cleavage of alkene: A new strategy to construct a mitochondria-targeted fluorescent probe for hydrogen peroxide imaging in vitro and in vivo / 烯烃的氧化切割：一种构建线粒体靶向荧光探针的新策略，用于体外和体内的过氧化氢成像
- 原肠运动：224 篇；Tetrabromobisphenol S (TBBPS)-induced developmental toxicity in zebrafish embryos: impacts on cytoskeletal proteins during gastrulation. / 四溴双酚S（TBBPS）诱导斑马鱼胚胎发育毒性：胃体发育过程中对细胞骨架蛋白的影响。；Transcriptional divergence of the zebrafish sox17 lineage begins during gastrulation. / 斑马鱼sox17谱系的转录分化始于原胃发育阶段。；BMP-dependent patterning of ectoderm tissue material properties modulates lateral mesendoderm cell migration during early zebrafish gastrulation. / 基于BMP的外胚层组织材料特性模式化调节了斑马鱼早期胃体化过程中侧向中胚层细胞迁移。；Endodermal cells use contact inhibition of locomotion to achieve uniform cell dispersal during zebrafish gastrulation / 内胚层细胞通过接触抑制运动来实现斑马鱼胃体化过程中细胞均匀扩散；Hoxb genes determine the timing of cell ingression by regulating cell surface fluctuations during zebrafish gastrulation. / Hoxb基因通过调节斑马鱼消化过程中细胞表面的波动来决定细胞进入的时间。
- 体轴建立：54 篇；C-mannosyltransferase Dpy19l1l regulates body axis formation via secretion of SCO-spondin in zebrafish / C-甘露基转移酶Dpy19l1l通过分泌SCO蛋白调节斑马鱼体轴的形成；Midnolin maintains zebrafish body axis stability and a progressive neuromuscular regulatory cascade. / 米德诺林维持斑马鱼体轴稳定性和渐进的神经肌肉调节级联反应。；Unravelling the progression of the zebrafish primary body axis with reconstructed spatiotemporal transcriptomics. / 通过重建的时空转录组学解析斑马鱼主要体轴的进展。；C-mannosyltransferase DPY19L1L-mediated Reissner Fiber formation is critical for zebrafish (Danio rerio) body axis straightening. / C-甘露基转移酶DPY19L1L介导的赖斯纳纤维形成对斑马鱼（Danio rerio）体轴直直至关重要。；A hydraulic feedback loop between mesendoderm cell migration and interstitial fluid relocalization promotes embryonic axis formation in zebrafish. / 中胚层细胞迁移与间质液重新定位之间的水力反馈环促进斑马鱼胚胎轴的形成。
- 体节发生：74 篇；Endocardial identity is established during early somitogenesis by Bmp signalling acting upstream of npas4l and etv2. / 在早期体节形成期间，通过Bmp信号在npas4l和etv2上游作用来确定心内膜身份。；Fgf8 dynamics and critical slowing down may account for the temperature independence of somitogenesis / Fgf8动力学和临界减速可能解释体体发生的温度独立性；Light Microscopy-based Elastography for the Mechanical Characterization of Zebrafish Somitogenesis. / 基于光学显微镜的弹性成像技术，用于机械表征斑马鱼体体生成。；Noise-resistant developmental reproducibility in vertebrate somite formation. / 脊椎动物体节形成中的抗噪声发育重复性。；斑马鱼mcm5基因的表达分析及在体节发生中的功能

- 颅面发育：84 篇；Functional and Genetic Analyses Unveil the Implication of hoxa4a in Zebrafish Craniofacial Development. / 功能和遗传分析揭示了hoxa4a在斑马鱼颅面发育中的意义。；The hedgehog co-receptors cdon and boc function redundantly to regulate zebrafish craniofacial development. / 刺猬共受体cdon和boc在调节斑马鱼颅面发育方面发挥冗余功能。；ctdsp2 Knockout Induces Zebrafish Craniofacial Dysplasia via p53 Signaling Activation / ctdsp2 敲除通过p53信号激活诱发斑马鱼颅面发育不良；Role of TGF-β3 in Regulating Neural Crest Cell Fate and Craniofacial Development: Insights From Zebrafish Models. / TGF-β3在调控神经嵴细胞命运和颅面发育中的作用：斑马鱼模型的洞见。；amer1 Regulates Zebrafish Craniofacial Development by Interacting with the Wnt/β-Catenin Pathway / amer1 通过与Wnt/β-链蛋白通路相互作用，调控斑马鱼颅面发育

- 下颌发育：15 篇；The Gq/11 family of Gα subunits is necessary and sufficient for lower jaw development. / Gq/11家族的Gα亚基对于下颌发育是必要且充分的。；The novel role of foxi3 in zebrafish mandibular development. / foxi3在斑马鱼下颌发育中的新颖作用。；耳-下颌发育不全综合征家系致病变异的筛选和功能研究；双侧小耳畸形患者的听力干预及耳-下颌发育不全综合征家系致病基因的筛选和功能研究；Abstract P22: Requirement Of talin1 For Cell Proliferation During Palate And Mandibular Development In Zebrafish / 摘要 P22：斑马鱼腭和下颌发育期间细胞增殖所需的talin1
- 颅骨：34 篇；Skeletal remodeling is responsible for the foramen in the skull for the hyoid artery in zebrafish. / 骨骼重塑负责斑马鱼舌骨动脉颅骨孔的形成。；Loss of Atoh8 Affects Neurocranial and Axial Skeleton Development in Zebrafish / Atoh8的丧失影响斑马鱼的神经颅骨和轴骨发育；Knockdown of best1 Gene in Zebrafish Caused Abnormal Neuronal and Skeletal Development -A Subtype of Craniovertebral Junction Malformation. / 斑马鱼最佳1基因的敲低导致神经元和骨骼发育异常——这是颅骨顶结处畸形的一个亚型。；成年斑马鱼颅骨缺损再生的光学相干层析成像研究；Non-destructive and high-resolution monitoring of inflammation-type skull defects regeneration on adult zebrafish with optical coherence tomography. / 利用光学相干断层扫描对成年斑马鱼炎症型颅骨缺损再生进行非破坏性和高分辨率监测。

### 肝脏、胰腺与胆道发育

- 肝脏发育：74 篇；1,6-Dihydroxynaphthalene as a potential environmental obesogen induces lipid accumulation and liver developmental defects in zebrafish / 1,6-二羟基萘作为潜在环境肥胖物，诱导斑马鱼脂质积累和肝脏发育缺陷；Bisphenol analogs affected hepatic development and disrupted lipid homeostasis in zebrafish larvae. / 双酚类似物影响了斑马鱼幼体的肝脏发育并破坏了脂质稳态。；Fenazaquin impairs liver development and metabolic homeostasis in zebrafish (Danio rerio) by disrupting glucose and lipid metabolism. / 芬那扎昆通过干扰葡萄糖和脂质代谢，损害斑马鱼（Danio rerio）的肝脏发育和代谢稳态。；Grna knockout resists HFD-induced obesity and leads to impaired liver development in zebrafish. / Grna敲除能抵抗高脂蛋白诱发的肥胖，并导致斑马鱼肝脏发育受损。；Mitochondrial protein Mrpl13 regulates zebrafish liver development through the mTORC1-mitochondrial homeostasis pathway. / 线粒体蛋白Mrpl13通过mTORC1-线粒体稳态通路调节斑马鱼肝脏的发育。
- 胰腺发育：55 篇；Hypusinated and unhypusinated isoforms of the translation factor eIF5A exert distinct effects in models of pancreas development and function. / 翻译因子eIF5A的上颌和非高羟异构体在胰腺发育和功能模型中具有不同作用。；RNA-Binding Protein Rbm38 as a Multifaceted Post-Transcriptional Regulator in Zebrafish Pancreatic Development. / RNA结合蛋白Rbm38作为斑马鱼胰腺发育中多面质后转录调控因子。；520 nm and 660 nm light-emitting diodes modulates pancreatic development and beta cell functions in zebrafish embryos. / 520 nm 和 660 nm 的发光二极管调节斑马鱼胚胎中的胰腺发育和 β 细胞功能。；Leveraging zebrafish to investigate pancreatic development, regeneration, and diabetes. / 利用斑马鱼研究胰腺发育、再生和糖尿病。；Melatonin protects zebrafish pancreatic development and physiological rhythms from sodium propionate-induced disturbances via the hypothalamic-pituitary-thyroid axis. / 褪黑素通过下丘脑-垂体-甲状腺轴保护斑马鱼胰腺发育和生理节律，防止丙酸钠引起的紊乱。
- 胆道发育：13 篇；Deletion of interleukin enhancer binding factor 2 (ILF2) resulted in defective biliary development and bile flow blockage / 白介素增强子结合因子2（ILF2）缺失导致胆道发育异常和胆汁流动阻塞；Biliary Tract Diseases and Conditions - Biliary Atresia; Researchers at University of Pennsylvania Release New Data on Biliary Atresia (Loss of a Candidate Biliary Atresia Susceptibility Gene, add3a, Causes Biliary Developmental Defects in Zebrafish) / 胆道疾病与状况——胆道闭锁;宾夕法尼亚大学研究人员发布了关于胆道闭锁的新数据（缺失候选胆道闭锁易感基因add3a，导致斑马鱼胆道发育缺陷）；Loss of a Candidate Biliary Atresia Susceptibility Gene, add3a, Causes Biliary Developmental Defects in Zebrafish. / 候选胆道闭锁易感基因add3a的丧失会导致斑马鱼胆道发育缺陷。；Mutations in vacuolar H + -ATPase subunits lead to biliary developmental defects in zebrafish / 液泡H-ATP酶亚基突变导致斑马鱼胆道发育缺陷；Intrahepatic biliary anomalies in a patient with Mowat-Wilson syndrome uncover a role for the zinc finger homeobox gene zfhx1b in vertebrate biliary development. / Mowat-Wilson综合征患者肝内胆道异常揭示了锌指同源盒基因zfhx1b在脊椎动物胆道发育中的作用。
- 肝细胞分化：3 篇；Hhex and Prox1a synergistically dictate the hepatoblast to hepatocyte differentiation in zebrafish. / Hhex和Prox1a协同决定了斑马鱼中肝母细胞到肝细胞的分化。；Roles for Notch‐Sox9 in biliary epithelial cells‐to‐hepatocytes differentiation during liver regeneration / Notch-Sox9在肝脏再生过程中胆道上皮细胞向肝细胞分化中的作用；缺失mir122抑制斑马鱼肝脏前体细胞向肝细胞分化
- 胰岛发育：6 篇；Investigating the role of dachshund b in the development of the pancreatic islet in zebrafish / 研究腊肠犬b在斑马鱼胰岛发育中的作用；Single-cell analyses identify distinct and intermediate states of zebrafish pancreatic islet development. / 单细胞分析识别斑马鱼胰岛发育的不同和中间状态。；An insulin signaling feedback loop regulates pancreas progenitor cell differentiation during islet development and regeneration / 胰岛素信号反馈回路调节胰腺前体细胞在胰岛发育和再生过程中的分化；Assessment of Toxicological Perturbations and Variants of Pancreatic Islet Development in the Zebrafish Model / 斑马鱼模型中胰腺胰岛发育的毒理扰动和变异评估；Targeted inhibition of miRNA maturation with morpholinos reveals a role for miR-375 in pancreatic islet development. / 通过形态素靶向抑制miRNA成熟，揭示了miR-375在胰岛发育中的作用。
- 内胚层器官发生：1 篇；13-P060 The role of Wnt signalling in endodermal organogenesis in zebrafish / 13-P060 斑马鱼内胚层器官生成中Wnt信号的作用

### 神经发育与轴突导向

- 神经管发育：4 篇；Pcgf1 Regulates Early Neural Tube Development Through Histone Methylation in Zebrafish / Pcgf1通过组蛋白甲基化调控斑马鱼早期神经管发育；Proteins - Nucleoproteins; Studies from University of Shandong Provide New Data on Nucleoproteins (SOX19b regulates the premature neuronal differentiation of neural stem cells through EZH2-mediated histone methylation in neural tube development of zebrafish) / 蛋白质- 核蛋白;山东大学的研究提供了关于核蛋白的新数据（SOX19b通过EZH2介导的组蛋白甲基化调控斑马鱼神经管发育中的神经干细胞早期分化）；SOX19b regulates the premature neuronal differentiation of neural stem cells through EZH2-mediated histone methylation in neural tube development of zebrafish. / SOX19b通过EZH2介导的组蛋白甲基化调控斑马鱼神经管发育中神经干细胞的过早神经分化。；miR-430 regulates oriented cell division during neural tube development in zebrafish / miR-430调控斑马鱼神经管发育过程中的定向细胞分裂
- 神经发生：250 篇；Astrocyte activation mediates UV filter PBSA-induced impairment of neurogenesis via the rxrga/tsp-1 axis during a sensitive developmental window in early life stage zebrafish. / 星形胶质细胞的活化介导紫外线滤镜（PBSA）通过rxrga/tsp-1轴诱导的神经发生损伤，这一过程发生在斑马鱼早期生命阶段的敏感发育窗口期。；Correction to "Epigallocatechin Gallate and Punicalagin Combination Reduces Aβ Aggregation and Promotes Neurogenesis in Adult Zebrafish Brain". / 更正“表食子儿茶素胆酸和Punicalagin联合使用减少Aβ聚集并促进成年斑马鱼大脑神经发生”。；Epigallocatechin Gallate and Punicalagin Combination Reduces Aβ Aggregation and Promotes Neurogenesis in Adult Zebrafish Brain. / 表半食子儿茶素没食子酸没食子酸和Punicalagin的组合可减少Aβ聚集，促进成年斑马鱼大脑的神经发生。；Physical activity stimulates neurogenesis via sensory neuron activity in postembryonic zebrafish / 体力活动通过感觉神经元活动刺激胚胎后斑马鱼的神经发生；Pmpcb modulates zebrafish neurogenesis and stress resistance via regulating mitochondria metabolism and function / PMPCB通过调节线粒体的代谢和功能，调节斑马鱼的神经发生和抗应激能力
- 神经嵴：313 篇；Cranial neural crest cells in zebrafish: Insights into craniofacial skeletal development and malformation. / 斑马鱼的颅神经嵴细胞：关于颅面骨骼发育与畸形的见解。；Her9 is required for the migration, differentiation, and survival of neural crest cells. / Her9对于神经嵴细胞的迁移、分化和存活至关重要。；miR-133a-3p and miR-338-3p shape neural crest derivatives in zebrafish. / miR-133a-3p 和 miR-338-3p 在斑马鱼中形成神经嵴衍生物。；Mutations in KAT3 family of lysine acetyl transferases impair neural crest migration in Rubinstein Taybi syndrome models. / 赖氨酸乙酰转移酶KAT3家族的突变在鲁宾斯坦·泰比综合征模型中会抑制神经嵴迁移。；PRDM3 and PRDM16 define cranial neural crest cell states in zebrafish development. / PRDM3和PRDM16定义了斑马鱼发育中的颅神经嵴细胞状态。
- 轴突导向：45 篇；自闭症风险基因tbr1调节轴突导向因子表达的机制研究；Molecular insights of axon guidance abnormalities in a mucopolysaccharidosis type II zebrafish model / 黏多糖贮积II型斑马鱼模型中轴突引导异常的分子见解；Mucopolysaccharidosis type II zebrafish model exhibits early impaired proteasomal-mediated degradation of the axon guidance receptor Dcc / 黏多糖贮积II型斑马鱼模型表现出蛋白酶体介导的早期功能障碍，轴突引导受体Dcc的降解；Rab11 promotes single Mauthner cell axon regeneration in vivo through axon guidance molecule Ntng2b. / Rab11通过轴突导引分子Ntng2b促进单Mauthner细胞轴突在体内再生。；Rab11通过轴突导向分子Ntng2b促进斑马鱼幼鱼毛特纳运动神经元轴突再生
- 轴突生长：65 篇；Toxic Effects of CdTe Quantum Dots on Locomotor Behavior, Neurodevelopment, and Axon Growth in Zebrafish Larvae. / CdTe量子点对斑马鱼幼虫运动行为、神经发育和轴突生长的毒性影响。；Developmental exposure of zebrafish to saxitoxin causes altered expression of genes associated with axonal growth / 斑马鱼发育暴露于萨克斯毒素会导致与轴突生长相关基因表达改变；Post-fertilization 2-ethylhexyl-4-methoxycinnamate (EHMC) exposure affects axonal growth, muscle fiber length, and motor behavior in zebrafish embryos. / 受精后2-乙基己基-4-甲氧基肉桂酸酯（EHMC）暴露会影响斑马鱼胚胎的轴突生长、肌纤维长度和运动行为。；Ethylene thiourea exposure induces neurobehavioral toxicity in zebrafish by disrupting axon growth and neuromuscular junctions / 乙烯硫脲暴露通过破坏轴突生长和神经肌肉连接，诱导斑马鱼的神经行为毒性；Stat3 Has a Different Role in Axon Growth During Development Than It Does in Axon Regeneration After Injury. / Stat3在发育过程中的轴突生长中的作用与在受伤后轴突再生中的作用不同。
- 髓鞘形成：65 篇；Deletion of wfs1 Impairs Oligodendrocyte Precursor Cells Dorsal Distribution and Myelination Through the wfs1-hmgcs1 Axis in Zebrafish / wfs1的缺失会损害斑马鱼中少突胶质细胞的背侧分布和髓鞘化，通过wfs1-hmgcs1轴；Nanoscale magnesium hydride alleviates hypoxia-induced myelination deficits in zebrafish. / 纳米级氢化镁可以缓解斑马鱼缺氧引起的髓鞘形成缺陷。；The chaperonin TRiC component Cct3 is required for axonal transport, myelination, and neuromuscular junction refinement. / 伴护蛋白TRiC组分Cct3对于轴突运输、髓鞘形成和神经肌肉连接处精细化是必需的。；The Microbiota Shapes Central Nervous System Myelination in Early Life. / 微生物群在早期生命中塑造中枢神经系统的髓鞘形成。；Amyloid-β Dysregulates Oligodendroglial Lineage Cell Dynamics and Myelination via PKC in the Zebrafish Spinal Cord. / 淀粉样蛋白β通过PKC调节斑马鱼脊髓中寡突三胶体谱系细胞动力学和髓鞘化。
- 运动神经元发育：21 篇；N-Acetylcysteine Prevents Arsenic-Induced Apoptosis but Not Supernumerary Motor Neuron Development in Zebrafish Embryos: Assessment of Protein Carbonylation and the p53 Pathway / N-乙酰半胱氨酸能防止砒霜诱导的凋亡，但不能阻止斑马鱼胚胎中多余的运动神经元发育：蛋白质羰基化及p53通路的评估；Perfluorooctanoic acid (PFOA) impairs motor neuron development and locomotor behavior in zebrafish larvae via ROS-mediated oxidative stress / 全氟辛酸（PFOA）通过ROS介导的氧化应激，损害斑马鱼幼体的运动神经元发育和运动行为；Acetyl L-Carnitine Protects Zebrafish Embryos From Verapamil and Inorganic Arsenic-Induced Cardiotoxicity and Developmental Toxicity With No Effect on Supernumerary Motor Neuron Development. / 乙酰左旋肉碱保护斑马鱼胚胎免受维拉帕米和无机砷诱发的心脏毒性和发育毒性，且不影响多余运动神经元发育。；srp54 promotes motor neuron development and is required for motility in zebrafish. / srp54促进运动神经元发育，是斑马鱼运动所必需的。；Enhancement of motor neuron development and function in zebrafish by sialyllacto-N-tetraose b. / 通过唾液乳糖-N-四氧化酶b增强斑马鱼运动神经元发育和功能。

### 突触与神经递质

- 突触形成：25 篇；Ephedrine-disrupted synaptogenesis signaling and behavioral abnormalities in adult zebrafish / 麻黄碱干扰的突触生成及成年斑马鱼的行为异常；Circadian regulation of developmental synaptogenesis via the hypocretinergic system. / 通过下肾酸碱系统调节发育突触发生的昼夜节律。；Perfluorononanoic Acid Induces Neurotoxicity via Synaptogenesis Signaling in Zebrafish. / 全氟丙烯酸通过突触发生信号在斑马鱼中诱导神经毒性。；16pdel lipid changes in iPSC-derived neurons and function of FAM57B in lipid metabolism and synaptogenesis / iPSC来源神经元中的16pdel脂质变化及FAM57B在脂质代谢和突触形成中的功能；An Experimental Approach to Study the Effects of Realistic Environmental Mixture of Linuron and Propamocarb on Zebrafish Synaptogenesis / 一种实验方法研究现实环境中林尿素和丙碳酸酯混合物对斑马鱼突触发生的影响
- 突触可塑性：12 篇；生慧汤调控cAMP/PKA/CREB/BDNF信号通路改善阿尔茨海默病斑马鱼模型突触可塑性和认知功能障碍的作用机制；Exposure to malathion impairs learning and memory of zebrafish by disrupting cholinergic signal transmission, undermining synaptic plasticity, and aggravating neuronal apoptosis. / 暴露于马拉硫磷会破坏斑马鱼的学习和记忆，因为它会干扰胆碱能信号传递，削弱突触可塑性，并加重神经元凋亡。；Extracellular matrix proteolysis maintains synapse plasticity during brain development. / 细胞外基质蛋白水解在大脑发育过程中维持突触可塑性。；Corrigendum to "Graphene quantum dots (GQDs) induce thigmotactic effect in zebrafish larvae via modulating key genes and metabolites related to synaptic plasticity" [Toxicology 487 (2023) 1-13]. / 更正指出“石墨烯量子点（GQDs）通过调节与突触可塑性相关的关键基因和代谢物，诱导斑马鱼幼体的硅形运动效应”[毒理学 487 （2023） 1-13]。；Graphene quantum dots (GQDs) induce thigmotactic effect in zebrafish larvae via modulating key genes and metabolites related to synaptic plasticity / 石墨烯量子点（GQDs）通过调节与突触可塑性相关的关键基因和代谢物，诱导斑马鱼幼体的声动运动效应
- 多巴胺：402 篇；5-O-甲基维斯阿米醇苷对6-羟基多巴胺诱导斑马鱼帕金森病模型中的保护作用；6PPD and its metabolites induce locomotor dysfunction in zebrafish through dopaminergic disruption with brain accumulation. / 6PPD及其代谢产物通过多巴胺能紊乱导致斑马鱼的运动功能障碍，伴随脑部积累。；Biphasic effects of 17β-estradiol and xenoestrogens on dopaminergic neurons in developing zebrafish / 17β-雌二醇和异种雌激素对发育中的斑马鱼多巴胺神经元的双相效应；Comparing the Role of Dopamine D1 and D3 Receptors in Mediating Aversive Learning in Zebrafish / 比较斑马鱼中多巴胺D1和D3受体在介导厌恶性学习中的作用；Early motor deficits, sleep dysfunction and reduction in dopaminergic neurons in a PARK7-/- zebrafish larval model of Parkinson's disease. / 早期运动功能障碍、睡眠功能障碍及多巴胺能神经元减少，基于PARK7-/-斑马鱼幼体模型。
- 血清素：212 篇；Bisphenol S causes deficits in social behaviour by disrupting serotonergic and BDNF-CREB1 signaling pathways. / 双酚S通过破坏血清素和BDNF-CREB1信号通路，导致社交行为缺陷。；Intestinal serotonin signaling suppression: A novel mechanism for overfeeding exacerbation by nanoplastics and MC-LR in zebrafish. / 肠道血清素信号抑制：一种新颖机制，用于纳米塑料和MC-LR在斑马鱼中过度喂食加重。；Isothiocyanate-Rich Essential Oil of Morisonia flexuosa L. Exhibits Anxiolytic-like Effects That May Involve Serotonergic Pathways in Zebrafish / Morisonia flexuosa L. 富含异硫氰酸酯的精油表现出类似抗焦虑作用，可能涉及斑马鱼的血清素能通路；Parental Bisphenol S exposure induces oxidative stress and disrupts serotonergic and cholinergic neurotransmission in zebrafish offspring. / 亲本暴露双酚S会诱发斑马鱼后代的氧化应激，并破坏血清素能和胆碱能神经传导。；Role of the serotonin 2C receptor in anxietylike behavior in zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 5-羟色胺2C受体在斑马鱼（Danio rerio）焦虑样行为中的作用。
- 谷氨酸能：110 篇；基于转录物组学探讨GABA能与谷氨酸能信号通路在甲卡西酮诱导斑马鱼成瘾行为中的作用（英文）；Cyhalothrin induced neurotoxicity via disrupting NF-κB and L-glutamate signaling and functions in zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 环虫蟃胓通过破坏斑马鱼中NF-κB和L-谷氨酸的信号传导及功能，诱导神经毒性（Danio rerio）。；Encapsulating gold nanoclusters into zinc-glutamate metal organic frameworks for ferric ions detection in zebrafish embryos. / 将金纳米簇封装在锌谷氨酸金属有机框架中，用于斑马鱼胚胎中铁离子检测。；Esketamine Exposure Affects Neurodevelopment and Glutamate-NMDAR Signaling in Zebrafish Larvae. / 琥珀胺暴露影响斑马鱼幼虫的神经发育和谷氨酸NMDAR信号传导。；Insights from zebrafish models of glutamatergic and GABAergic dysregulation in neurological and psychiatric disorders. / 来自斑马鱼模型关于神经和精神疾病中谷氨酸能和GABA能失调的见解。
- GABA能：125 篇；基于转录物组学探讨GABA能与谷氨酸能信号通路在甲卡西酮诱导斑马鱼成瘾行为中的作用（英文）；Bakuchiol, a potentially toxic component in Fructus Psoraleae, causes liver damage in vitro by affecting the homeostasis of the liver GABA signalling system. / 巴库酚是紫果叶中的一种潜在有毒成分，通过影响肝脏GABA信号系统的稳态，在体外造成肝损伤。；Discovery of GABA-containing cyclic peptides from the cold-seep-derived fungus Aspergillus unguis SCSIO 41322 for the treatment of inflammatory bowel disease. / 发现来自冷渗真菌Aspergillus unguis SCSIO 41322的含GABA环肽，用于治疗炎症性肠病。；Disruption of GABAergic and GH/IGF-related signaling is associated with growth inhibition induced by dibutyl phthalate and dibutyl adipate in zebrafish. / GABA能和GH/IGF相关信号的破坏与斑马鱼中邻苯二甲酸丁酯和二丁基二硫二酸引起的生长抑制有关。；Enhancing the GABA Content and Sleep-Promoting Potential of the Baihe Dihuang Decoction Through Lactic Acid Bacteria Fermentation / 通过乳酸菌发酵提升白合滴丸的GABA含量和助眠潜力
- 胆碱能：113 篇；Combined Aluminum Chloride and Lead Acetate Exposure Induces Anxiety-like Behavior, Cognitive Impairment, and Cholinergic–Oxidative Dysregulation in Adult Zebrafish: Optimization of a Dementia-like Model / 氯化铝和醋酸铅联合暴露诱发成年斑马鱼的焦虑样行为、认知障碍及胆碱能-氧化失调：痴呆样模型的优化；Crosstalk with the cholinergic pathway may be responsible for chlorpyrifos-induced reproductive toxicity in fish / 胆碱能通路的交互作用可能导致鱼类中氯吡芦诱导的生殖毒性；Non-target aquatic safety of the botanical insecticide candidate Combretum trifoliatum and its major constituent camphor: Sex-dimorphic neurotoxicity, cholinergic disruption, and water persistence in zebrafish. / 植物杀虫剂候选物Combretum trifoliatum及其主要成分樟脑的非靶向水生安全性：斑马鱼的性别二态性神经毒性、胆碱能干扰及水质持久性。；Parental Bisphenol S exposure induces oxidative stress and disrupts serotonergic and cholinergic neurotransmission in zebrafish offspring. / 亲本暴露双酚S会诱发斑马鱼后代的氧化应激，并破坏血清素能和胆碱能神经传导。；Photocontrol of zebrafish behavior with a photoswitchable ligand of nicotinic acetylcholine receptors. / 利用尼古丁乙酰胆碱受体的光开关配体调控斑马鱼行为。

### 痛觉、感觉神经与成瘾

### 神经发育与精神疾病

- 自闭症谱系障碍：135 篇；Alpha-lipoic acid attenuates valproic acid-induced autism-like phenotypes in zebrafish by enhancing antioxidant capacity and repressing oxidative stress, inflammation, apoptosis, and HPI-axis signaling. / α-硫辛酸通过增强抗氧化能力并抑制氧化应激、炎症、凋亡和HPI轴信号，减弱斑马鱼中由丙戊酸诱导的自闭症样表型。；Autism Spectrum Disorder: High-Resolution Elucidation of Mitochondrial Dysregulation in Larval Zebrafish Gut / 自闭症谱系障碍：高分辨率阐明斑马鱼幼体肠道线粒体失调；Brain-wide circuitry underlying altered auditory habituation in zebrafish models of autism. / 斑马鱼自闭症模型中听觉习惯化改变的大脑全脑回路。；Exploratory evaluation of the neuroprotective potential of Alfalfa in an AlCl₃-induced autism-like zebrafish model. / 在AlCl诱导的类斑马鱼模型中，Alfalfa的神经保护潜力探索性评估。；Fecal microbiota transplantation and Akkermansia muciniphila restore neurodevelopment and behavior via the gut-brain axis in autism-like zebrafish. / 粪便微生物群移植和Akkermansia muciniphila通过肠脑轴恢复自闭症斑马鱼的神经发育和行为。
- 注意缺陷多动障碍：22 篇；Exploring Zebrafish Models of ADHD: A Systematic Review of Genetic and Pharmacological Approaches. / 探索斑马鱼多动症模型：遗传学与药理学方法的系统综述。；Butylated Hydroxyanisole (BHA) Disrupts Brain Signalling in Embryo-Larval Stage of Zebrafish Leading to Attention Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD). / 丁基羟异醇（BHA）在斑马鱼胚幼虫阶段干扰脑信号传导，导致注意力缺陷多动障碍（ADHD）。；Early-life microplastic exposure elicits ADHD-like behaviors by disrupting dopaminergic neurodevelopment in zebrafish. / 早期生命中的微塑料暴露通过破坏斑马鱼的多巴胺神经发育，引发类似多动症的行为。；Embryonic exposure of estrogen and BPA in zebrafish leads to ADHD-like and ASD-like phenotypes, respectively. / 斑马鱼胚胎暴露雌激素和BPA分别导致类似ADHD和ASD样表型。；A new mechanistic insight into the association between environmental perfluorooctane sulfonic acid (PFOS) exposure and attention deficit and hyperactivity disorder (ADHD)-like behavior. / 对环境全氟辛烷磺酸（PFOS）暴露与注意力缺陷障碍及多动障碍（ADHD）样行为之间的关联的新机制见解。
- 精神分裂症：41 篇；A ketamine/methionine exposure paradigm, alone or combined with Lactobacillus rhamnosus GG, assessing schizophrenia-relevant behavioral, oxidative stress, and molecular endpoints in adult zebrafish. / 一种氯胺酮/蛋氨酸暴露范式，单独或与Lactobacillus rhamnosus GG联合，评估成年斑马鱼中与精神分裂症相关的行为、氧化应激和分子终点。；In situ nasal gel loaded with Lactoferrin-Coated Brexpiprazole nanostructured lipid carriers for Schizophrenia: Cross-Species validation in Ketamine-Induced rat and zebrafish models. / 装载含有乳铁蛋白涂层布雷非哌唑纳米结构脂质载体的原位鼻腔凝胶用于治疗精神分裂症：在氯胺酮诱导大鼠和斑马鱼模型中的跨物种验证。；Zebrafish screen of schizophrenia risk genes reveals convergent dysregulation of cholesterol metabolism. / 斑马鱼筛查精神分裂症风险基因，显示胆固醇代谢趋同失调。；Preliminary Data Regarding the Alleviating Effects of Haloperidol and Risperidone on the Short-Term Memory and Associative Learning in a Zebrafish Model of Schizophrenia / 关于氟哌啶醇和利培酮在精神分裂症斑马鱼模型中短期记忆和联想学习缓解效果的初步数据；A Functional Schizophrenia-associated genetic variant near the TSNARE1 and ADGRB1 genes. / 一个功能性精神分裂症相关遗传变异，位于TSNARE1和ADGRB1基因附近。
- 双相情感障碍：6 篇；The dopamine transporter inhibition using GBR 12909 as a novel pharmacological tool to assess bipolar disorder-like neurobehavioral phenotypes in zebrafish. / 利用GBR 12909作为一种新型药理学工具，用于评估斑马鱼类双相情感障碍样神经行为表型的多巴胺转运蛋白抑制。；环丙沙星引起斑马鱼双相情感障碍的毒性作用机制研究；Multi-omics analysis of a drug-induced model of bipolar disorder in zebrafish / 斑马鱼药物诱导双相情感障碍模型的多组学分析；Zebrafish as a potential non-traditional model organism in translational bipolar disorder research: genetic and behavioral insights / 斑马鱼作为转化型双相情感障碍研究中的潜在非传统模式生物：遗传与行为洞见；Zebra Fish (Danio rerio) Diving into Bipolar Disorder / 斑马鱼（Danio rerio）潜入双相情感障碍
- 智力障碍：28 篇；Emerging role of KDM5C in X-linked intellectual disability based on human genetic data and zebrafish models / 基于人类遗传数据和斑马鱼模型，KDM5C在X连锁智力障碍中的新兴作用；儿童智力障碍相关的AFF2基因突变型-表型相关性研究及突变型功能验证；Bi-allelic KICS2 mutations impair KICSTOR complex-mediated mTORC1 regulation, causing intellectual disability and epilepsy. / 双等位基因KICS2突变会损害KICSTOR复合物介导的mTORC1调控，导致智力障碍和癫痫。；Bi-allelic MED16 variants cause a MEDopathy with intellectual disability, motor delay, and craniofacial, cardiac, and limb malformations. / 双等位基因MED16变异会导致MEDopathy，伴有智力障碍、运动迟缓以及颅面、心脏和肢体畸形。；Loss of Function SPTAN1 Variants Result in Ataxia and Intellectual Disability. / 功能丧失SPTAN1变异导致共济失调和智力障碍。
- 脆性X综合征：9 篇；Electrical synapses mediate embryonic hyperactivity in a zebrafish model of Fragile X syndrome. / 在脆弱X综合征斑马鱼模型中，电突触介导胚胎过度活跃。；Loss of FMRP affects ovarian development and behaviour through multiple pathways in a zebrafish model of fragile X syndrome. / 在斑马鱼脆弱X综合征模型中，FMRP丧失通过多条通路影响卵巢发育和行为。；Zebrafish Chromosome 14 Gene Differential Expression in the fmr1hu2787 Model of Fragile X Syndrome / 斑马鱼14号染色体在fmr1hu2787脆性X综合征模型中的基因差异表达；A DNAzyme based knockdown model for Fragile-X syndrome in zebrafish reveals a critical window for therapeutic intervention / 基于DNA酶的斑马鱼脆性X综合征敲低模型揭示了治疗干预的关键窗口；Altered brain-wide auditory networks in a zebrafish model of fragile X syndrome. / 斑马鱼脆弱X综合征模型中脑域听觉网络的改变。

### 癫痫与神经损伤

- 癫痫：447 篇；[Effects and activity of paeoniflorin in zebrafish model of epilepsy induced by pentylenetetrazol]. / [戊二四醇诱发癫痫斑马鱼模型中paeoniflorin的作用和活性]。；癫痫相关基因chd2在斑马鱼胚胎中的时空表达特征；芍药苷在戊四唑诱导斑马鱼癫痫模型中的作用以及活性研究；Antiepileptic potential of salicylhydroxamic acid: genomic, molecular docking, and functional evaluations in pentylenetetrazole-induced adult zebrafish / 水杨酰羟胺酸的抗癫痫潜力：戊二四唑诱导成年斑马鱼的基因组学、分子结合及功能评估；Anxiolytic and Anticonvulsant Effects of Moringa Isothiocyanate-1 (MIC-1) in Adult Zebrafish / 辣木异硫氰酸酯-1（MIC-1）在成年斑马鱼中的抗焦虑和抗癫痫作用
- 惊厥：207 篇；Deficiency of pnkp in zebrafish causes microcephaly, seizures, and developmental delay through mitochondrial dysfunction. / 斑马鱼缺乏PNKP会导致小头畸形、癫痫发作以及线粒体功能障碍导致发育迟缓。；Elevated body temperature exacerbates arrhythmia and seizure-like activity in a zebrafish model of Timothy syndrome. / 体温升高会加重斑马鱼蒂莫西综合征模型中的心律失常和类似癫痫的活动。；Embryonic Lead Acetate Exposure Induces Seizure-like Activity in Zebrafish Larvae / 胚胎期的醋酸铅暴露会引发斑马鱼幼虫的类似癫痫发作的活动；Escape behaviors are transiently modulated after acutely induced epileptic seizures in larval zebrafish. / 幼体斑马鱼在急性诱发癫痫发作后，逃逸行为会短暂性地调节。；Fast volumetric imaging of a zebrafish seizure model with adaptive optics light sheet microscopy. / 利用自适应光学光学片显微镜对斑马鱼癫痫发作模型进行快速体积成像。
- 脑缺血：13 篇；天麻素干预急性脑缺血再灌注损伤及促血管新生的调控机制研究；Development and Standardization of a Simple Zebrafish Larval Model of Global Hypoxia-Reoxygenation: Recapitulating Key Pathological Features Associated with Cerebral Ischemia-Reperfusion Injury. / 开发和标准化简单斑马鱼幼体全球缺氧-再氧化模型：重现与脑缺血-再灌注损伤相关的关键病理特征。；醒脑静注射液对脑缺血-再灌注损伤并发脑心综合征的保护作用研究；Reinwardtia indica Dumort. protects against ponatinib-induced cerebral ischemia in adult zebrafish via attenuation of oxidative stress, neuroinflammation, mitochondrial dysfunction, and neurotransmitter dysregulation: A multi-target therapeutic strategy. / 印度德兰迪亚·杜莫特。通过减弱氧化应激、神经炎症、线粒体功能障碍和神经递质失调，保护成年斑马鱼免受波拿替尼诱发的脑缺血：一种多靶点治疗策略。；基于线粒体MCU通道探究甲基莲心碱促血管再生抗脑缺血的作用机制
- 脑损伤：75 篇；Arbutin mediated neuroprotection in zebrafish model of traumatic brain injury via modulating Nrf2/NF-κB pathway / 在斑马鱼模型中，通过调节Nrf2/NF-κB通路介导的神经保护；Compromised blood-brain barrier in traumatic brain injury model of Danio rerio: A unique window to demonstrate restoration of behavioral, cellular, and neurochemical deficits by limonin. / Danio rerio创伤性脑损伤模型中血脑屏障受损：展示利莫宁恢复行为、细胞和神经化学缺陷的独特窗口。；Correction: Arbutin mediated neuroprotection in zebrafish model of traumatic brain injury via modulating Nrf2/NF-κB pathway / 更正：阿巴巴丁通过调节Nrf2/NF-κB通路，介导了斑马鱼创伤性脑损伤模型中的神经保护；High-Precision Pneumatic Induction of Traumatic Brain Injury in Larval Zebrafish. / 《斑马鱼幼虫创伤性脑损伤的高精度气动诱导》。；High-Precision Pneumatic Induction of Traumatic Brain Injury in Larval Zebrafish. / 《斑马鱼幼虫创伤性脑损伤的高精度气动诱导》。
- 兴奋性毒性：5 篇；A Potential Neurotoxic Mechanism: Bisphenol S-Induced Inhibition of Glucose Transporter 1 Leads to ATP Excitotoxicity in the Zebrafish Brain. / 一种潜在的神经毒性机制：双酚S诱导葡萄糖转运蛋白1的抑制导致斑马鱼大脑中的ATP兴奋性毒性。；Novel S1R agonists counteracting NMDA excitotoxicity and oxidative stress: A step forward in the discovery of neuroprotective agents. / 新型S1R激动剂抵消NMDA兴奋性毒性和氧化应激：神经保护剂发现的一大进步。；Prophylactic Activation of Shh Signaling Attenuates TBI-Induced Seizures in Zebrafish by Modulating Glutamate Excitotoxicity through Eaat2a / 通过调节谷氨酸兴奋毒性，预防性激活Shh信号，减轻斑马鱼TBI引起的癫痫发作；Ca2+-Permeable AMPARs Mediate Glutamatergic Transmission and Excitotoxic Damage at the Hair Cell Ribbon Synapse. / Ca2——可渗透的AMPARs介导谷氨酸能传递和毛细胞带突触的兴奋性毒性损伤。；A Model of Excitotoxic Brain Injury in Larval Zebrafish: Potential Application for High-Throughput Drug Evaluation to Treat Traumatic Brain Injury. / 斑马鱼幼体兴奋性毒性脑损伤模型：在高通量药物评估中治疗创伤性脑损伤的潜在应用。
- 神经元损伤：7 篇；Amlodipine Attenuates Carrageenan-induced Oxidative Stress Targeting Transsynaptic Neuronal Damage by Promoting Survival of Retinal Ganglion Cells in Adult Zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 氨氯地平通过促进成年斑马鱼视网膜神经节细胞的存活，减弱卡拉胶诱导的氧化应激，靶向跨突触神经元损伤。；脑脉泰胶囊对外周神经元损伤模型斑马鱼神经再生的影响；Dysregulation of Microglial Function Contributes to Neuronal Impairment in Mcoln1a-Deficient Zebrafish / 小胶质功能失调导致Mcoln1a缺乏斑马鱼的神经元损伤；Acteoside protects against 6-OHDA-induced dopaminergic neuron damage via Nrf2-ARE signaling pathway / 肌苷通过Nrf2-ARE信号通路保护6-OHDA诱导的多巴胺神经元损伤；斑马鱼神经元损伤模型在养血清脑颗粒质量控制中的应用分析

### 视网膜与视觉疾病

- 视网膜发育：105 篇；Disrupted Copper Homeostasis and Impaired Retinal Development Caused by slc6a4a Deficiency in Zebrafish / 斑马鱼中因slc6a4a缺乏导致铜稳态紊乱和视网膜发育受损；Elevated PMMA levels in dental clinic wastewater induce retinal developmental toxicity in zebrafish by disrupting dhrs9-mediated retinol metabolism. / 牙科诊所废水中PMMA水平升高，通过干扰DHRS9介导的视黄醇代谢，诱发斑马鱼的视网膜发育毒性。；Investigating the Role of Zebrafish Retinoschisin Homologs Rs1a and Rs1b During Retinal Development. / 研究斑马鱼视黄膜裂开蛋白同源蛋白Rs1a和Rs1b在视网膜发育中的作用。；Maternal-Derived Polystyrene Nanoplastics Impair Early Retinal Development in Zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 母体来源的聚苯乙烯纳米塑料阻碍斑马鱼（Danio rerio）视网膜早期发育。；Mechanism and toxicity of hydroxychloroquine-induced retinal developmental impairment and locomotor behavioral alterations in zebrafish larvae / 羟氯喹诱导的视网膜发育障碍及斑马鱼幼体运动行为改变的机制与毒性
- 视觉发育：15 篇；视觉发育早期单色光长期暴露对斑马鱼幼鱼光谱偏好性的干扰效应；Pannexin 1a regulates visual system development and ocular integrity in zebrafish / Pannexin 1a调控斑马鱼的视觉系统发育和眼部完整性；Role of Glutamate Receptor-Related Genes in Myopia Occurrence and Visual Development. / 谷氨酸受体相关基因在近视发生和视觉发育中的作用。；The novel PFOS substitute OBS induces visual developmental toxicity in zebrafish embryos via ferroptosis. / 新型PFOS替代OBS通过铁消亡诱导斑马鱼胚胎的视觉发育毒性。；视觉发育早期单色光长期暴露对斑马鱼光谱偏好性和焦虑响应的干扰效应
- 视觉功能：143 篇；Adult zebrafish favor vision over flow sensing in increasingly complex hydrodynamic environments. / 成年斑马鱼在日益复杂的水动力学环境中更倾向于视觉而非流动感知。；AquaMaze: A Computer Vision-Based Framework for Automated Behavioral Analysis of Zebrafish in Controlled Environments / AquaMaze：基于计算机视觉的框架，用于受控环境中斑马鱼的自动行为分析；Behavioral Vision Testing Using an Automated Observational Tracking System (VisioBox) to Obtain a Measure of Visual Acuity in Larval Zebrafish. / 使用自动观察追踪系统（VisioBox）进行行为视力测试，以测量斑马鱼幼体的视力。；Comparative phenotypic analysis of rhodopsin supports stepwise evolution of dim-light vision in early vertebrates. / 视紫红质的比较表型分析支持早期脊椎动物昏暗视觉的逐步进化。；Dual Knockout Models of the Spatially and Functionally Conserved rgra and rgrb Zebrafish Genes Reveal the Requirement of RGR for the Integrity of Cone-Mediated Photopic Vision, the Photopic Visual Cycle and Bruch's Membrane Morphology. / 空间和功能保守的rgra和rgrb斑马鱼基因的双重敲除模型揭示了RGR对于锥细胞介导的光视视觉完整性、光视视觉周期和布鲁赫膜形态学的完整性需求。
- 视觉损伤：16 篇；DIAPH1 regulates the Wnt/β-catenin pathway resulting in microcephaly and visual impairment. / DIAPH1调控Wnt/β-链蛋白通路，导致小头畸形和视力障碍。；Exposure to 4-hydroxy-4′-isopropoxydiphenylsulfone causes retinal structural damage and visual impairment in zebrafish / 暴露于4-羟基-4′-异丙氧二苯基磺酮会导致斑马鱼视网膜结构损伤和视力障碍；Juvenile-onset visual impairment in zebrafish rlbp1b mutants highlights Müller glia contributions to cone vision. / 斑马鱼RLBP1b突变体的幼年性视觉障碍突出了穆勒胶质细胞对锥体视觉的贡献。；Parental ochratoxin A exposure induces multigenerational and transgenerational neurotoxicity and visual impairment in zebrafish. / 亲代赭毒素A暴露会引发斑马鱼多代及跨代神经毒性及视觉障碍。；菟丝子通过调控光转导通路改善嘧菌胺诱导的斑马鱼视觉损伤及潜在活性成分鉴定（英文）
- 视神经发育：3 篇；跨膜前后轴转换1蛋白结构及其在视神经发育中的功能研究；Stmn4敲除斑马鱼胚胎内视神经发育的研究；Deficient FGF signaling causes optic nerve dysgenesis and ocular coloboma. / FGF信号传导不足会导致视神经发育不良和眼部缺失。
- 光转导：21 篇；Genetic and Molecular Basis of Cone Opsin Phototransduction / 锥细胞视蛋白光转导的遗传和分子基础；Visual neurotoxicity of zinc oxide nanoparticles on zebrafish larvae: Roles of retinal injury, redox imbalance, and impaired phototransduction. / 斑马鱼幼体氧化锌纳米颗粒的视觉神经毒性：视网膜损伤、氧化还原失衡和光传导受损的作用。；菟丝子通过调控光转导通路改善嘧菌胺诱导的斑马鱼视觉损伤及潜在活性成分鉴定（英文）；Cuscutae Semen ameliorates mepanipyrim-induced visual impairment in zebrafish via the phototransduction pathway with identification of potential active constituents / Cuscutae精液通过光转导通路改善斑马鱼因甲儚引起的视觉障碍，并识别潜在活性成分；Effect of Dync1h1 on Phototransduction Protein Transport and the Development and Maintenance of Photoreceptor Cells in Zebrafish. / Dync1h1对斑马鱼光转导蛋白运输及光感受细胞发育与维持的影响。
- 青光眼：25 篇；TNFα-dependent modulation of WT1-MMP9 regulatory axis links developmental and inflammatory pathways in glaucoma / TNFα依赖的WT1-MMP9调控轴调节青光眼发育和炎症通路；Genomic Analysis of Glaucoma Pathogenesis Due to gmds Mutation in Zebrafish. / 斑马鱼中gmds突变导致青光眼发病机制的基因组分析。；Orphan odyssey: zebrafish-derived bio-actives in multi-target networks for rare retinal ganglion cell degenerations mimicking glaucomatous optic neuropathy. / 孤儿奥德赛：斑马鱼衍生的生物活性物质在多靶点网络中用于罕见视网膜神经节细胞变性，模拟青光眼性视神经病变。；Association between glaucoma susceptibility with combined defects in mitochondrial oxidative phosphorylation and fatty acid beta oxidation / 青光眼易感性与线粒体氧化磷酸化与脂肪酸β氧化的联合缺陷之间的关联；ATXN2 loss of function results in glaucoma-related features supporting a role for Ataxin-2 in primary open-angle glaucoma (POAG) pathogenesis. / ATXN2功能丧失导致青光眼相关特征出现支持Ataxin-2在原发性开放角青光眼（POAG）发病机制中的作用。
- 黄斑变性：11 篇；Emerging organophosphate flame retardant CDP causes neovascular macular degeneration-like alterations of outer blood-retinal barrier via paracrine VEGFA signaling / 新兴的有机磷阻燃剂CDP通过旁分泌VEGFA信号引发新生血管黄斑变性样的外血视网膜屏障改变；Marine-Derived Fucoidan Modulates Pathways Associated with Age-Related Macular Degeneration in Cellular and Zebrafish Models. / 海洋来源的富考丹调节细胞和斑马鱼模型中与年龄相关黄斑变性的通路。；Chrysanthemum morifolium Extract Attenuates Pathological Angiogenesis of Age-Related Macular Degeneration via VEGF and Nrf2 Pathway Modulation. / 菊花提取物通过VEGF和Nrf2途径调节，减缓年龄相关性黄斑变性的病理血管生成。；Phenotypic Drug Discovery Platform by Quantitative High-Throughput Screening Identifies Antiapoptotic Molecules in a Zebrafish Model of Age-Related Macular Degeneration. / 通过定量高通量筛选，表型药物发现平台在年龄相关黄斑变性斑马鱼模型中识别出抗凋亡分子。；Exploring the effect of intracellular cholesterol mis-trafficking in age-related macular degeneration (AMD) models / 探讨细胞内胆固醇误输在老年相关黄斑变性（AMD）模型中的影响
- 视网膜色素变性：63 篇；Zebrafish Functional Screening of FDA-Approved Drugs for Autosomal Dominant Retinitis Pigmentosa Caused by RHODOPSIN Q344X Mutation. / 斑马鱼功能性筛查FDA批准药物，检测由视紫红质Q344X突变引起的常染色体显性视网膜色素变性。；Corrigendum to "Corrigendum to Cortex Dictamni induces retinitis pigmentosa in zebrafish by inhibiting pde6a post-transcriptional activity via mmu-mir-6240-p3_2" [J. Ethnopharmacol. 340 (2025) 119282]. / 更正于“皮层更正通过MMU-mir-6240-p3_2抑制pde6a转录后活性，诱导斑马鱼视网膜色素变性”[《民族药学杂志》340（2025）119282]。；Do variants in the CDH23 gene cause non-syndromic retinitis pigmentosa? Dual validation using whole exome sequencing and a zebrafish model. / CDH23基因的变异是否会导致非综合征性色素变性视网膜？使用全外显子测序和斑马鱼模型进行双重验证。；Exploring exon excision as a therapeutic intervention strategy for the future treatment of ADGRV1-associated retinitis pigmentosa / 探索外显子切除作为ADGRV1相关视网膜色素变性未来治疗干预策略的探讨；Cortex Dictamni induces retinitis pigmentosa in zebrafish by inhibiting pde6a post-transcriptional activity via mmu-mir-6240-p3_2. / 皮层Dictamni通过MMU-mir-6240-p3_2抑制pde6a转录后活性，诱导斑马鱼的视网膜色素变性。

- 光感受器：365 篇；A Dynamic Change of Microglial States Occurs During the Transition From Photoreceptor Degeneration to Regeneration in Zebrafish pde6c Mutants. / 斑马鱼PDE6c突变体在从感光器退化向再生过程中，微胶质状态会发生动态变化。；Cdhr1a and pcdh15b may link photoreceptor outer segments with calyceal processes revealing a potential mechanism for cone-rod dystrophy / Cdhr1a和pcdh15b可能将感光细胞外节与萼片突起连接，揭示锥杆神经营养不良的潜在机制；Cone photoreceptor ablation in microglia-deficient larval zebrafish retina elicits a regenerative response alongside a compensatory immune cell response. / 小胶质细胞缺乏的斑马鱼幼虫视网膜锥体感光细胞消融会引发再生反应，同时伴随代偿性免疫细胞反应。；Early-Life Exposure to InP/ZnS QDs Triggers Photoreceptor Apoptosis via ER Stress in Zebrafish: Implications for Nanomaterial-Induced Retinal Toxicity. / 早期暴露于InP/ZnS QDs通过内质网应激引发斑马鱼的光感受器凋亡：对纳米材料诱导视网毒性的启示。；Effect of permethrin insecticide on TRβ-mediated cone photoreceptor differentiation and spectral sensitivity in zebrafish larvae (Danio rerio). / 对TRβ介导的斑马鱼幼体锥细胞分化和光谱敏感性的影响（Danio rerio）。

### 听觉、毛细胞与侧线

- 听觉：231 篇；A Splice-Site Variant in ACY1 Associated with Congenital Hearing Loss: Clinical, Biochemical, and Zebrafish Functional Evidence / ACY1中与先天性听力损失相关的接合位点变异：临床、生化及斑马鱼功能证据；Behavioral and acetylcholinesterase responses to auditory enrichment in zebrafish exposed to conspecific alarm substance. / 暴露于同种警示物质的斑马鱼听觉丰富后的行为反应和乙酰胆碱酯酶反应。；Brain-wide circuitry underlying altered auditory habituation in zebrafish models of autism. / 斑马鱼自闭症模型中听觉习惯化改变的大脑全脑回路。；Expression Analysis of the TMC Gene Family in the Auditory System During Zebrafish Embryonic Development. / 斑马鱼胚胎发育期间听觉系统中TMC基因家族的表达分析。；GJB2 c.109G > A mutation activating IFI27-mediated mitochondrial apoptosis pathway leading to hereditary non-syndromic hearing loss. / GJB2 c.109G > 一种突变激活IFI27介导的线粒体凋亡通路，导致遗传性非综合征性听力损失。

- 前庭功能：5 篇；RIPOR2: A new gene of non-syndromic cochleovestibular dysfunction, discrepancy between human pathology and animal models. / RIPOR2：一种非综合征性耳蜗前庭功能障碍的新基因，即人类病理学与动物模型之间的差异。；An Assay for Systematically Quantifying the Vestibulo-Ocular Reflex to Assess Vestibular Function in Zebrafish Larvae. / 一种系统性定量前庭-眼反射以评估斑马鱼幼虫前庭功能的检测法。；Zebrafish pax5 regulates development of the utricular macula and vestibular function. / 斑马鱼pax5调控房膜黄斑和前庭功能的发育。；A critical period for functional vestibular development in zebrafish. / 斑马鱼前庭功能发育的关键时期。；Development of utricular otoliths, but not saccular otoliths, is necessary for vestibular function and survival in zebrafish. / 形成心房耳石，而非囊状耳石，是斑马鱼前庭功能和生存所必需的。

- 味觉：39 篇；Developing zebrafish utilize taste-signaling pathways for oxygen chemoreception. / 发育中的斑马鱼利用味觉信号通路进行氧气化学感受。；A singular shark bitter taste receptor provides insights into the evolution of bitter taste perception. / 一种独特的鲨鱼苦味受体为苦味感知的演化提供了见解。；Bitter taste receptors of the zebra finch (Taeniopygia guttata) / 斑胸草雀（Taeniopygia guttata）的苦味受体；Expression of taste sentinels, T1R, T2R, and PLCß2, on the passageway for olfactory signals in zebrafish. / 斑马鱼嗅觉信号通道上的味觉哨兵T1R、T2R和PLCß2的表达。；Vimentin Localization in the Zebrafish Oral Cavity: A Potential Role in Taste Buds Regeneration / 斑马鱼口腔中的维膜素定位：在味蕾再生中的潜在作用
- 嗅觉神经元：9 篇；Assaying sensory ciliopathies using calcium biosensor expression in zebrafish ciliated olfactory neurons. / 利用钙生物传感器表达在斑马鱼纤毛化嗅觉神经元中检测感觉纤毛病变。；Cell Surface Extensions - Cilia; New Findings from Massachusetts General Hospital Yields New Data on Cilia (Assaying sensory ciliopathies using calcium biosensor expression in zebrafish ciliated olfactory neurons) / 细胞表面延伸——纤毛;麻省总医院的新发现带来了关于睫毛的新数据（利用斑马鱼纤毛化嗅觉神经元中钙生物传感器表达检测感觉纤毛病变）；Differential copper-induced death and regeneration of olfactory sensory neuron populations and neurobehavioral function in larval zebrafish / 铜诱导的嗅觉神经元群体和幼体神经行为功能的差异性死亡与再生；Exposure to Zinc Sulfate Results in Differential Effects on Olfactory Sensory Neuron Subtypes in Adult Zebrafish / 暴露于硫酸锌对成年斑马鱼嗅觉神经元亚型产生差异效应；Shedding new light on the origins of olfactory neurons. / 为嗅觉神经元的起源带来了新的启示。

### 心脏发育与先天性心脏病

- 心脏发育：377 篇；Corrigendum to 'Exposure to the Polychlorinated biphenyl mixture Aroclor 1254 elicits neurological and cardiac developmental effects in early life stage zebrafish (Danio rerio)' [Chemosphere 371 (2025), 144023]. / 更正指出“暴露于多氯联苯混合物Aroclor 1254会引发斑马鱼（Danio rerio）早期生命阶段的神经和心脏发育影响”[Chemosphere 371 （2025）， 144023]。；CRISPR/Cas9介导的斑马鱼gse1基因敲除及其对心脏发育的影响；Early-life exposure to flusilazole causes transgenerational cardiac developmental defects in zebrafish and its potential causes / 早年接触氟西拉唑会导致斑马鱼的跨代心脏发育缺陷及其潜在原因；ERα-mediated endoplasmic reticulum stress drives 4-tert-octylphenol-induced cardiac developmental toxicity in zebrafish. / ERα介导的内质网应激驱动斑马鱼中4-三甲辛酚诱导的心脏发育毒性。；HFPO-TA induces cardiac developmental damage in zebrafish by disrupting mitochondrial dynamics through FABP3 / HFPO-TA通过FABP3干扰线粒体动态，诱导斑马鱼的心脏发育损伤

- 造血发生：233 篇；Angptl5 restricts primitive hematopoiesis by promoting retinoic acid signaling in zebrafish. / Angptl5通过促进斑马鱼的视黄酸信号传导，限制原始造血。；Correction: A non-canonical function of zebrafish telomerase reverse transcriptase is required for developmental hematopoiesis. / 更正：斑马鱼端粒酶逆转录酶在发育造血中需要非典型功能。；Tracing developmental and adult hematopoiesis with an endogenous zebrafish runx1-2A-CreERT2 CRISPR knock-in. / 通过内源性斑马鱼runx1-2A-CreERT2 CRISPR敲入追踪发育和成体造血过程。；A zebrafish model of parn deficiency identifies altered lipid metabolism as a novel therapeutic target for restoring hematopoiesis in dyskeratosis congenita / 斑马鱼模型中的帕恩缺乏症发现，改变脂质代谢是恢复先天性角化病造血的新治疗靶点；BCAS2 promotes primitive hematopoiesis by sequestering β-catenin within the nucleus / BCAS2通过在核内封存β-链蛋白促进原始造血
- 红细胞生成：90 篇；MORC2 controls HIF-1α stability via an HDAC4-dependent mechanism to regulate erythropoiesis. / MORC2通过依赖HDAC4的机制调控HIF-1α的稳定，以调节红细胞生成。；p4htm acts as a non-redundant regulator of HIF signaling to govern erythropoiesis and renal homeostasis in zebrafish. / p4htm作为非冗余的HIF信号调控因子，调控斑马鱼的红细胞生成和肾脏稳态。；TKI-mediated inhibition of NLRP1 inflammasome restores erythropoiesis in DBA syndrome / TKI介导的NLRP1炎症小体抑制恢复DBA综合征中的红细胞生成；斑马鱼胚胎发育中促红细胞生成素epoa及其受体epor的表达；Characterization of zebrafish alas2 reporter for study on erythropoiesis, blood flow and erythroid disease. / 对斑马鱼alas2报告者的特征描述，用于红细胞生成、血流和红细胞疾病研究。
- 髓系发生：40 篇；Bisphenol A and its analogs perturb primitive myelopoiesis and inhibit innate immune cell formation during early developmental stages of zebrafish. / 双酚A及其类似物扰乱原始的脊髓生成，并在斑马鱼早期发育阶段抑制先天免疫细胞的形成。；Zebrafish: a convenient tool for myelopoiesis research. / 斑马鱼：一种方便的髓生成研究工具。；3082 – NONALCOHOLIC STEATOHEPATITIS PROMOTES EMERGENCY MYELOPOIESIS AND IMMUNE EFFECTOR DYSFUNCTION / 3082 – 非酒精性脂肪性肝炎促进紧急骨髓生成和免疫功能障碍；Telomerase RNA recruits RNA polymerase II to target gene promoters to enhance myelopoiesis. / 端粒酶RNA招募RNA聚合酶II以定位基因启动子，以增强髓生成作用。；Macrophage-Derived IL-1β Regulates Emergency Myelopoiesis via the NF-κB and C/ebpβ in Zebrafish. / 巨噬细胞来源的IL-1β通过斑马鱼的NF-κB和C/ebpβ调控紧急骨髓生成。
- 淋巴细胞发生：7 篇；CFTR regulates embryonic T lymphopoiesis via Wnt signaling in zebrafish / CFTR通过斑马鱼的Wnt信号调控胚胎T淋巴生成；Ikzf1 regulates embryonic T lymphopoiesis via Ccr9 and Irf4 in zebrafish. / Ikzf1通过Ccr9和Irf4调控斑马鱼胚胎T淋巴生成。；Novel regulators of lymphopoiesis identified using forward genetic screens in zebrafish / 通过斑马鱼的前向遗传筛选识别出淋巴细胞生成的新调控因子；The first wave of T lymphopoiesis in zebrafish arises from aorta endothelium independent of hematopoietic stem cells / 斑马鱼的第一波T淋巴生成波起源于主动脉内皮，独立于造血干细胞；The first wave of T lymphopoiesis in zebrafish arises from aorta endothelium independent of hematopoietic stem cellsDistinct waves of T lymphopoiesis in zebrafish / 斑马鱼的第一波T淋巴生成波源自主动脉内皮，独立于造血干细胞。斑马鱼中T淋巴生成的明显波
- 血细胞分化：1 篇；Gfi1aa/Lsd1 Facilitates Hemangioblast Differentiation Into Primitive Erythrocytes by Targeting etv2 and sox7 in Zebrafish / Gfi1aa/LSD1通过靶向斑马鱼中的etv2和sox7，促进血细胞分化为原始红细胞

### 贫血、凝血与血栓

- 贫血：92 篇；Altered translation efficiency of specific mRNAs in a zebrafish model of Diamond-Blackfan anemia syndrome. / 斑马鱼模型中特定mRNA翻译效率的变化。；Gcn2 deficiency triggers anemia and hypoxic intolerance in zebrafish. / Gcn2缺乏会引发斑马鱼的贫血和缺氧不耐受。；Steamed Panax notoginseng saponins ameliorate cyclophosphamide-induced anemia by promoting erythroid progenitor proliferation via the cAMP/PI3K/AKT/cGMP pathway. / 蒸制的Panax notoginseng皂苷通过cAMP/PI3K/AKT/cGMP途径促进红细胞前体增殖，缓解环磷酰胺诱发的贫血。；先天性纯红细胞再生障碍性贫血实验模型研究进展；Genetic inactivation of FAAP100 causes Fanconi anemia due to disruption of the monoubiquitin ligase core complex. / FAAP100的遗传失活因单泛素连接酶核心复合物的破坏导致范可尼贫血。
- 凝血：67 篇；Dabigatran prevents lipopolysaccharide mediated apoptosis in zebrafish through a thrombin independent mechanism / 达比加特兰通过凝血酶非依赖机制防止斑马鱼脂多糖介导的凋亡；Deconstructing cardiovascular and coagulation-related traits links dietary ecology to multi-functional snake venom specificity. / 解构心血管和凝血相关性状，将饮食生态学与多功能蛇毒的特异性联系起来。；Identification of berbamine hydrochloride as a novel coagulation factor VII inhibitor by natural product library screening using zebrafish as a model. / 通过以斑马鱼为模型，通过天然产物库筛选，鉴定了苯胺盐酸盐作为一种新型凝血因子VII抑制剂。；Attenuation of rotenone-induced neurodegeneration by Bacillus coagulans supplementation in zebrafish. / 斑马鱼中补充凝血杆杆菌对回替酮诱导神经退行性的减缓。；Dabigatran prevents lipopolysaccharide mediated apoptosis in zebrafish through a thrombin independent mechanism. / 达比加特兰通过与凝血酶无关的机制，防止斑马鱼脂多糖介导的凋亡。
- 血栓形成：183 篇；丹参-红花抗血栓效应的配伍优化及物质基础研究；地龙提取物减轻炎症缓解斑马鱼血栓形成；A sensitized model of thrombosis validates known multigenic relationships and suggests novel modifiers of hemostasis. / 一个增敏化的血栓模型验证了已知的多基因关系，并提出了止血的新修饰因子。；Development of a quality evaluation card for saffron (Crocus sativus L.) by integrating chemical, colorimetric, and antithrombotic bioactivity analyses / 通过整合化学、比色和抗血栓生物活性分析，开发藏红花（Crocus sativus L.）质量评估卡；In vivo screening of Thai plant extracts for Antithrombocyte activity using zebrafish: Discovery of novel antiplatelet compounds. / 利用斑马鱼检测泰国植物提取物抗血栓细胞活性的体内检测：发现新型抗血小板化合物。
- 出血：111 篇；基于斑马鱼模型探究synpo在颅内出血中的作用机制；跨膜转运蛋白zgc:158423功能缺失导致氧化磷酸化功能紊乱诱发脑出血；c-AMP 141靶向VE-cadherin/β-catenin恢复血管内皮细胞屏障功能以挽救出血性脑卒中；CIGB-258, a Potential Novel Approach to Treat Sepsis-like Hyperinflammation, Reduces Gastrointestinal Hemorrhage in Zebrafish Exposed to Carboxymethyllysine and Ethanol / CIGB-258是一种潜在的新颖治疗败血症样高炎症方法，能减少暴露于羧甲基赖氨酸和乙醇的斑马鱼的胃肠道出血；Comparison of Anti-Acute Phase Effect of CIGB-258 and Its Wild-Type Peptide (E18-3) in a Hyperinflammatory and Acute Bleeding Model of Zebrafish: A Surface Plasmon Resonance Study to Compare Binding Affinity with High-Density Lipoproteins / 比较CIGB-258及其野生型肽（E18-3）在斑马鱼高炎症急性出血模型中的抗急性效应：一项表面等离子体共振研究，比较高密度脂蛋白的结合亲和力
- 血小板：93 篇；遗传性血小板减少症动物模型研究进展；An Inducible Zebrafish Model of Thrombocytopenia Enables In Vivo Drug Screening and Reveals FGF Signaling as a Key Regulator of Platelet Regeneration. / 诱导性斑马鱼血小板减少模型支持体内药物筛选，并揭示FGF信号作为血小板再生的关键调控因子。；In vivo screening of Thai plant extracts for Antithrombocyte activity using zebrafish: Discovery of novel antiplatelet compounds. / 利用斑马鱼检测泰国植物提取物抗血栓细胞活性的体内检测：发现新型抗血小板化合物。；Cloche/Npas4l is a pro-regenerative platelet factor during zebrafish heart regeneration. / Cloche/Npas4l是一种促进斑马鱼心脏再生过程中血小板再生因子。；Defensin-Rich Platelets Drive Pro-Tumorigenic Programs in Pancreatic Adenocarcinoma / 富含防御素的血小板驱动胰腺腺癌的促进肿瘤项目
- 凝血因子：15 篇；Identification of berbamine hydrochloride as a novel coagulation factor VII inhibitor by natural product library screening using zebrafish as a model. / 通过以斑马鱼为模型，通过天然产物库筛选，鉴定了苯胺盐酸盐作为一种新型凝血因子VII抑制剂。；Knockdown of tmem183a in Zebrafish Causes Thrombocytopenia and Reduces Coagulation Factors, Disrupting Hemostasis. / 在斑马鱼中敲低tmem183a会导致血小板减少，降低凝血因子，破坏止血。；Rare crocins ameliorate thrombus in zebrafish larvae by regulating lipid accumulation and clotting factors. / 稀有的番针素通过调节脂质积累和凝血因子，减轻斑马鱼幼体中的血栓。；Upregulation of coagulation factor V by glucocorticoid in the preovulatory follicles of zebrafish. / 斑马鱼排卵前卵泡中糖皮质激素上调凝血因子V。；OC 45.2 Knockdown of Zebrafish Tmem242 Enhances Production of ROS thatSignals to Increase Foxa2 and Foxa3 Expression that May Increase Clotting Factors Resulting in Thrombosis and Bleeding / OC 45.2 敲低斑马鱼Tmem242增强ROS的产生，从而信号增加Foxa2和Foxa3表达，进而增加凝血因子，进而引发血栓和出血

### 白血病与血液肿瘤

- 急性髓系白血病：39 篇；Application and characterisation of four genetically engineered animal models (mouse/zebrafish/rhesus monkey/Drosophila) in acute myeloid leukemia. / 四种基因工程动物模型（小鼠/斑马鱼/恒河猴/果蝇）在急性髓系白血病中的应用与特性分析。；Phenazine 5,10-dioxide analogues as potential therapeutics in AML: Efficacy on patient-derived blasts, in zebrafish larvae xenografts and synergy with venetoclax. / 苯甲酸5,10-二氧化物类似物作为AML潜在治疗手段：对患者来源囊胚、斑马鱼幼体异种移植及与维奈托克莱的协同疗效。；Targeting acute myeloid leukemia resistance with two novel combinations demonstrate superior efficacy in TP53, HLA-B, MUC4 and FLT3 mutations. / 针对急性髓系白血病耐药的两种新组合显示出TP53、HLA-B、MUC4和FLT3突变的优越疗效。；In Vivo Chemical Screening in Zebrafish Embryos Identified FDA-Approved Drugs That Induce Differentiation of Acute Myeloid Leukemia Cells / 在斑马鱼胚胎中的体内化学筛选发现了FDA批准的药物，这些药物能诱导急性髓系白血病细胞的分化；The Rac1-inhibitor EHop-016 attenuates AML cell migration and enhances the efficacy of daunorubicin in MOLM-13 transplanted zebrafish larvae / Rac1抑制剂EHop-016抑制AML细胞迁移，并增强DAUNORUBICIN在移植斑马鱼幼虫中的疗效
- 急性淋巴细胞白血病：131 篇；All-in-one, Cas13d-based cell-specific gene knockdown system for zebrafish. / 一体化、基于Cas13d的细胞特异性基因敲低系统，适用于斑马鱼。；Imaging cellular activity across all organs reveals body-wide circuits / 通过成像检查所有器官的细胞活动，可以发现全身的回路；Ultra-fast generation of all-female grass carp via transplantation of female germline stem cells into zebrafish / 通过将雌性生殖系干细胞移植到斑马鱼体内，实现全雌性草鲤的超快速生成；A novel stable transgenic zebrafish line expressing mCherry-tagged human alpha-synuclein in the nervous system and exhibiting all the key features of Lewy body disorders at larval stage. / 一种新型稳定转基因斑马鱼系，在神经系统中表达mCherry标记的人类α-突触核蛋白，并在幼虫阶段表现出路易体疾病的所有关键特征。；Acvr2b receptors transduce all BMP signaling in the zebrafish gastrula and restrict Fibrodysplasia Ossificans Progressiva ACVR1-R206H signaling in a dose-dependent manner. / Acvr2b受体在斑马鱼胃管中转导所有BMP信号，并以剂量依赖性方式限制纤维异形成性骨化型（Progressiva ACVR1-R206H）信号。
- 白血病：184 篇；A transgenic zebrafish leukemia model driven by KMT2A::MLLT3 (MLL-AF9) / 由KMT2A：：MLLT3（MLL-AF9）驱动的转基因斑马鱼白血病模型；CBX4 enhances acute monocytic leukemia development via HDAC-mediated suppression of Runx1. / CBX4通过HDAC介导的Runx1抑制，促进急性单细胞白血病的发展。；1015 – AHR REGULATES MYELOID CELL DEVELOPMENT AND DIFFERENTIATION VIA CNR2 AND ENHANCES ATRA-INDUCED DIFFERENTIATION AND PROMOTES CYTOTOXIC RESPONSE IN LEUKEMIA CELLS / 1015 – AHR通过CNR2调控髓系细胞的发育和分化，增强ATRA诱导的分化，并促进白血病细胞的细胞毒性反应；Application and characterisation of four genetically engineered animal models (mouse/zebrafish/rhesus monkey/Drosophila) in acute myeloid leukemia. / 四种基因工程动物模型（小鼠/斑马鱼/恒河猴/果蝇）在急性髓系白血病中的应用与特性分析。；Effects of realgar-indigo naturalis formula on a zebrafish tumor xenograft model induced by human acute promyelocytic leukemia cells: antitumor activity, hepatotoxicity, and transcriptomic analysis / 皇家黄靛蓝自然蛋白配方对人类急性原骨髓细胞白血病诱导的斑马鱼肿瘤异种移植模型的影响：抗肿瘤活性、肝毒性及转录组分析
- 淋巴瘤：11 篇；Evaluating indole‑gold(I) based complexes as potential anti lymphoma agents by disrupting the thioredoxin reductase/glutathione peroxidase axis. / 通过破坏硫氧还蛋白还原酶/谷胱甘肽过氧化物酶轴，评估基于吲哚-金（I）的复合物作为潜在的抗淋巴瘤药物。；Constitutively active CaMKII Drives B lineage acute lymphoblastic leukemia/lymphoma in tp53 mutant zebrafish. / 持续活性的CaMKII驱动T53突变斑马鱼中的B谱系急性淋巴细胞白血病/淋巴瘤。；B cell lymphoma 6A regulates immune development and function in zebrafish / B细胞淋巴瘤6A调控斑马鱼的免疫发育和功能；IRF4 drives clonal evolution and lineage choice in a zebrafish model of T-cell lymphoma / IRF4驱动斑马鱼模型中的克隆进化和谱系选择；Influence of oncogenic mutations and tumor microenvironment alterations on extranodal invasion in diffuse large B-cell lymphoma. / 致癌突变和肿瘤微环境改变对弥漫性大B细胞淋巴瘤节外侵袭的影响。
- 骨髓增生异常：8 篇；人源骨髓增生异常综合征肿瘤细胞斑马鱼模型构建及益髓颗粒干预研究；Minoxidil ameliorates myelodysplastic syndrome by targeting Wnt4 while sparing normal hematopoiesis. / 米诺地尔通过靶向Wnt4缓解脊髓增生异常综合征，同时保留正常造血。；骨髓增生异常综合征在斑马鱼中的研究进展；Assessment of a novel NRAS in-frame tandem duplication causing a myelodysplastic/myeloproliferative neoplasm. / 评估一种新型NRAS帧内串联复制导致骨髓增生异常/骨髓增生性肿瘤。；Depletion of tet2 results in age-dependent changes in DNA methylation and gene expression in a zebrafish model of myelodysplastic syndrome / tet2的耗竭会导致斑马鱼骨髓增生异常综合征模型中DNA甲基化和基因表达随年龄变化
- 骨髓增殖性肿瘤：3 篇；Assessment of a novel NRAS in-frame tandem duplication causing a myelodysplastic/myeloproliferative neoplasm. / 评估一种新型NRAS帧内串联复制导致骨髓增生异常/骨髓增生性肿瘤。；Irf8 regulates the progression of myeloproliferative neoplasm-like syndrome via Mertk signaling in zebrafish. / Irf8通过斑马鱼中的Mertk信号调控骨髓增生性类肿瘤综合征的进展。；NUP98-HOXA9-transgenic zebrafish develop a myeloproliferative neoplasm and provide new insight into mechanisms of myeloid leukaemogenesis. / NUP98-HOXA9转基因斑马鱼会发展出骨髓增生性肿瘤，并为骨髓性白细胞生成的机制提供了新的见解。

### 先天免疫与炎症

- 先天免疫：214 篇；A core innate immune gene panel derived from germ-free larval zebrafish transcriptomics resolves strain-specific induction by Aeromonas veronii and Lacticaseibacillus rhamnosus GG. / 一个核心先天免疫基因面板，源自无菌斑马鱼幼体转录组学，解析了Aeromonas veronii和Lacticaseibacillus rhamnosus GG对菌株特异性诱导的问题。；Embryonic diazepam exposure induces innate immune toxicity in zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 胚胎期地西泮暴露会诱导斑马鱼（Danio rerio）先天免疫毒性。；Embryonic exposure to ambient concentrations of PFAS impairs the innate immune response in zebrafish and fathead minnow. / 胚胎阶段暴露于环境浓度的PFAS会损害斑马鱼和胖头鲦的先天免疫反应。；Environmental concentrations of Acetamiprid exposure induced innate immunotoxicity in zebrafish larvae via crosstalk between the TLR4/MYD88/NF-κB and JAK-STAT signaling pathways. / 环境中浓度的乙酰啉暴露通过TLR4/MYD88/NF-κB和JAK-STAT信号通路之间的交互作用，诱导斑马鱼幼体先天免疫毒性。；Flagellin pretreatment enhances host early innate immune responses against mycobacterial infection. / 鞭毛素预处理能增强宿主早期先天免疫对分枝杆菌感染的反应。
- 炎症反应：985 篇；3-n-Propionyl Zaluzanin C from Saussurea involucrata alleviates inflammation and oxidative stress: potential involvement of the NRF2 signaling pathway / 来自卷轴索休里亚的3-n-丙基扎鲁扎宁C可缓解炎症和氧化应激：NRF2信号通路的潜在参与；6PPD impairs liver growth through inflammatory pathways: Insights from zebrafish and human cell models. / 6PPD通过炎症途径损害肝脏生长：斑马鱼和人体细胞模型的见解。；地龙提取物减轻炎症缓解斑马鱼血栓形成；金松酸对斑马鱼MASLD炎症的调控作用；马铃薯皮提取物缓解多环境应激源诱导皮肤炎症的机制研究

- 皮质醇：116 篇；Biological Validation of Cortisol in Zebrafish Trunk, Skin Mucus, and Water as a Biomarker of Acute or Chronic Stress / 斑马鱼鼻、皮肤黏液和水中皮质醇作为急性或慢性应激生物标志物的生物学验证；Single exposure to Ayahuasca reverses chronic stress effects on sociability, anxiety, cortisol, and BDNF in zebrafish / 单次接触阿亚胡斯卡可逆转斑马鱼对社交能力、焦虑、皮质醇和BDNF的慢性压力影响；adgrl3.1 Knockout Disrupts Cortisol Regulation and Stress Reactivity, Linking Externalizing and Internalizing Behaviors. / adgrl3.1 敲除破坏皮质醇调节和应激反应，将外化和内化行为联系起来。；Blunted cortisol and altered antioxidant defense as biomarkers of systemic stress in hyperglycemic Zebrafish / 皮质醇减弱和抗氧化防御改变作为高血糖斑马鱼系统性应激的生物标志物；Differential effects of chronic unpredictable stress on behavioral and molecular (cortisol and microglia-related neurotranscriptomic) responses in adult leopard (leo) zebrafish. / 慢性不可预测压力对成年豹（Leo）斑马鱼的行为和分子（皮质醇和小胶质细胞相关神经转录组学）反应的差异性影响。

### 性别决定与性腺发育

- 性别决定：29 篇；Exposure to Glyphosate and Chlorpyrifos Induces Oxidative Stress, Potentially Impacting Sex Determination in Zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 暴露于草甘膦和氯吡虫萦会引发氧化应激，可能影响斑马鱼（Danio rerio）的性别决定。；Consequences of Cancer on Zebrafish Danio rerio: Insights Into Sex Determination, Sex Ratio, and Offspring Survival. / 癌症对斑马鱼的影响：性别决定、性别比与后代存活的见解。；布氏鲳鲹（Trachinotus blochii）性别决定候选基因hsd17β1的鉴定及功能研究；Cellular retinoic acid binding proteins regulate germ cell proliferation and sex determination in zebrafish. / 细胞视黄酸结合蛋白调控斑马鱼的生殖细胞增殖和性别决定。；The effects of 17α-methyltestosterone on gonadal histology and gene expression along hypothalamic-pituitary-gonadal axis, germ cells, sex determination, and hypothalamus-pituitary-thyroid axis in zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 17α-甲基睾酮对斑马鱼（Danio rerio）中性腺组织学和下丘脑-垂体-性腺轴的基因表达、生殖细胞、性别决定以及下丘脑-垂体-甲状腺轴的影响。
- 性分化：61 篇；Probiotic Administration Reshapes the Sex Differentiation Trajectory of Zebrafish under Perfluorobutanesulfonate Challenge. / 益生菌施用重塑斑马鱼在全氟丁烷磺酸盐挑战下的性别分化轨迹。；The Role of DNA Methylation and ZBTB Transcription Factors in Sex Differentiation, Maintenance and Heat‐Induced Masculinization in Zebrafish / DNA甲基化和ZBTB转录因子在斑马鱼性别分化、维持及热诱导雄性化中的作用；Timeline of sex differentiation and stabilization in zebrafish: Interplay between methyl-sensitive transcription factors, prostaglandin-related genes and DNA methylation. / 斑马鱼性别分化与稳定时间线：甲基敏感转录因子、前列腺素相关基因与DNA甲基化之间的相互作用。；Distinctively Different Effects of Perfluorobutanoic Acid and Perfluorononanoic Acid on Zebrafish Sex Differentiation and Androgen Receptor Activity. / 全氟丁酸和全氟丙烯酸对斑马鱼性别分化和雄激素受体活性有明显不同的影响。；The exposure to Nandrolone changed the expression of genes associated with sexual differentiation and disrupted the levels of hormones in the hypothalamic-pituitary-gonadal (HPG) axis, ultimately inducing male differentiation in zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 暴露于南德龙改变了与性别分化相关的基因表达，并扰乱了下丘脑-垂体-性腺（HPG）轴的激素水平，最终诱导了斑马鱼（Danio rerio）雄性分化。
- 性腺发育：54 篇；Cholecystokinin coordinates gonadotropin-dependent and independent pathways to orchestrate zebrafish gonadal development. / 胆囊激酶协调促性腺激素依赖和独立通路，以协调斑马鱼的性腺发育。；Knockout of hsd3b1 disrupts steroid hormone homeostasis and compromises gonadal development in zebrafish. / hsd3b1的敲除会破坏类固醇激素的稳态，损害斑马鱼的性腺发育。；镉暴露对斑马鱼幼鱼性腺发育毒性的细胞和分子效应研究；Impact of copper nanoparticles (CuNPs) on gonadal development in zebrafish larvae and melatonin therapeutic intervention. / 铜纳米颗粒（CuNPs）对斑马鱼幼体性腺发育的影响及褪黑素治疗干预。；Long-term exposure to environmentally relevant concentrations of tri-m-cresyl phosphate induces fecundity decline and gonadal dysplasia by disrupting reproductive endocrine homeostasis. / 长期暴露于环境相关浓度的三米基磷酸盐，通过破坏生殖内分泌稳态，导致生育能力下降和性腺发育不良。
- 卵巢发育：23 篇；Protocol for culture of zebrafishdeveloping ovaries as an ex vivo system for investigating early oogenesis and ovarian development. / 斑马鱼培养方案作为体外系统，用于研究早期卵子生成和卵巢发育。；Multi- and Transgenerational Histological and Transcriptomic Outcomes of Developmental TCDD Exposure in Zebrafish (Danio rerio) Ovary / 斑马鱼（Danio rerio）卵巢发育性TCDD暴露的多代及跨代组织学及转录组结局；尾加压素Ⅱ对斑马鱼卵巢发育的影响及其作用机制的研究；Loss of FMRP affects ovarian development and behaviour through multiple pathways in a zebrafish model of fragile X syndrome. / 在斑马鱼脆弱X综合征模型中，FMRP丧失通过多条通路影响卵巢发育和行为。；雄激素受体缺乏抑制肝脏Vtg合成导致卵巢发育障碍
- 睾丸发育：4 篇；Bisphenol A diglycidyl ether-induced DNA methylation abnormalities may disrupt testis development in adult male zebrafish. / 双酚A二甘基醚诱导的DNA甲基化异常可能干扰成年雄性斑马鱼的睾丸发育。；Stress responses of testicular development, inflammatory and apoptotic activities in male zebrafish ( Danio rerio ) under starvation / 雄性斑马鱼（Danio rerio）在饥饿条件下睾丸发育、炎症和凋亡活动的应激反应；A critical role of foxp3a-positive Treg cells in maintaining immune homeostasis in zebrafish testis development / foxp3a阳性Treg细胞在维持斑马鱼睾丸发育免疫稳态中的关键作用；Gonadotropin Signaling in Zebrafish Ovary and Testis Development: Insights From Gene Knockout Study. / 斑马鱼卵巢和睾丸发育中的促性腺激素信号传导：基因敲除研究的见解。
- 性逆转：18 篇；Recurrent ZC3H18 mutations stabilize oncogenic endogenous retroviral RNA / 反复出现的ZC3H18突变稳定了致癌内源性逆转录病毒RNA；Gag proteins encoded by endogenous retroviruses are required for zebrafish development. / 由内源性逆转录病毒编码的Gag蛋白是斑马鱼发育所必需的。；Recurrent oncogenic ZC3H18 mutations stabilize endogenous retroviral RNA. / 反复出现的致癌性ZC3H18突变稳定内源性逆转录病毒RNA。；Zglp-1 is a novel essential transcriptional regulator for sex reversal in zebrafish / ZGLP-1是一种新型的必备转录调控因子，用于斑马鱼性别逆转；Gag proteins encoded by endogenous retroviruses are required for zebrafish development. / 由内源性逆转录病毒编码的Gag蛋白是斑马鱼发育所必需的。

### 生育力、配子与受精

- 精子发生：80 篇；Clonal dynamics deviate from neutral drift in zebrafish spermatogenesis. / 斑马鱼精子生成中的克隆动力学偏离中性漂移。；LncRNA THOR regulates spermatogenesis by interacting with and stabilizing Sycp3 protein in zebrafish. / LncRNA THOR 通过与斑马鱼中的 Sycp3 蛋白相互作用并稳定其精子生成来调控。；A single-cell multiomics roadmap of zebrafish spermatogenesis reveals regulatory principles of male germline formation / 斑马鱼精子发生的单细胞多组学路线图揭示了雄性生殖系形成的调控原理；Arginine vasotocin is a player in the multifactorial control of spermatogenesis in zebrafish / 精氨酸凡索托辛是斑马鱼精子生成多因素调控的重要成分；Chronic Exposure to Glufosinate-ammonium Induces Male Reproductive Toxicity by Mitochondrial Impairment and spermatogenesis Disruption in Zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 长期暴露于葡萄糖铵铵会导致斑马鱼（Danio rerio）中的线粒体损伤和精子生成破坏导致男性生殖毒性。
- 卵子发生：58 篇；Exposure to Estrone Impairs Oogenesis and Spawning in Zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 暴露于雌激素会损害斑马鱼（Danio rerio）的卵生成和产卵。；Membrane Progestin Receptors α and β (Paqr7 and 8) are Necessary for Oogenesis in Zebrafish / 膜孕激素受体α和β（Paqr7和8）是斑马鱼卵生成的必要条件；Protocol for culture of zebrafishdeveloping ovaries as an ex vivo system for investigating early oogenesis and ovarian development. / 斑马鱼培养方案作为体外系统，用于研究早期卵子生成和卵巢发育。；Decoding zebrafish oogenesis: From primordial germ cell development to fertilization. / 斑马鱼卵生成解码：从原始生殖细胞发育到受精。；Impairment of zebrafish (Danio rerio) oogenesis and F1 embryonic development by maternal methylparaben exposure: Insights from transcriptomic analysis. / 母体甲基对羟基苯甲酸酯暴露导致斑马鱼（Danio rerio）卵发生损伤及F1胚胎发育：转录组分析的见解。
- 卵母细胞成熟：85 篇；Atrazine and diazinon inhibit oocyte maturation and ovulation in zebrafish. / 阿特拉津和二氮侘抑制斑马鱼的卵母细胞成熟和排卵。；Intestinal Accumulation of Polyester Microfibers Modulates HPG Axis Regulation and Oocyte Maturation in Zebrafish (Danio rerio) / 聚酯微纤维的肠道积累调节斑马鱼（Danio rerio）中HPG轴调节和卵母细胞成熟；Brain derived neurotrophic factor promotes oocyte maturation through the TrkB-MAPK-PI3K pathway in zebrafish / 脑源性神经营养因子通过斑马鱼的TrkB-MAPK-PI3K通路促进卵母细胞成熟；Brain Derived Neurotrophic Factor Stimulates Hypothalamic and Gonadal Reproductive Hormones and Oocyte Maturation in Zebrafish. / 脑源性神经营养因子刺激斑马鱼的下丘脑和性腺生殖激素以及卵母细胞成熟。；Endogenous biosynthesis of docosahexaenoic acid (DHA) regulates fish oocyte maturation by promoting pregnenolone production. / 内源性二十碳六烯酸（DHA）通过促进孕烯醇酮的产生来调节鱼卵母细胞成熟。
- 受精：134 篇；bucky ball mRNA is responsive to the total RNA content in the fertilized zebrafish oocyte / 巴基球mRNA对受精斑马鱼卵母细胞中的总RNA含量有反应；Decoding zebrafish oogenesis: From primordial germ cell development to fertilization. / 斑马鱼卵生成解码：从原始生殖细胞发育到受精。；The circadian clock orchestrates spermatogonial differentiation and fertilization by regulating retinoic acid signaling in vertebrates. / 昼夜节律通过调节脊椎动物的视黄酸信号，协调精子胚细胞的分化和受精。；A conserved fertilization complex bridges sperm and egg in vertebrates. / 一个保守的受精复合体连接着脊椎动物的精子和卵子。；Effect of Sperm Extender and Dilution Ratio on The Sperm Motility, Fertility, and Hatching Rates of Depik Fish Rasbora Tawarensis (Pisces: Cyprinidae) Eggs / 精子延长和稀释比对德皮克鱼（双鱼科）灯鱼（Rasbora tawarensis，双鱼科）卵子的运动性、受精率和孵化率的影响
- 生育力：58 篇；Combination of Policosanol (Raydel®) and Atorvastatin Improve High-Density Lipoproteins and Antioxidant Abilities to Protect Vital Organs and Fertility in Hyperlipidemic/Hyperglycemic Zebrafish / 聚氯胺醇（雷德尔®）和阿托伐他汀的组合能提升高脂蛋白和抗氧化能力，保护高脂血症/高血糖斑马鱼的重要器官和生育能力；Bisphenol AF (BPAF) jeopardizes male fertility and triggers intergenerational defects in zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 双酚AF（BPAF）危害雄性生育能力，并引发斑马鱼（Danio rerio）的代际缺陷。；Environmental concentrations of glyphosate through direct or parental exposure alter nervous system development and reduce the fertility rate in zebrafish. / 通过直接或亲代暴露导致的草甘膦环境浓度会改变斑马鱼的神经系统发育并降低其生育率。；Resveratrol attenuates ethylene glycol butyl ether-impaired zebrafish fertility via antioxidant mechanism. / 白藜芦醇通过抗氧化机制削弱乙二醇丁基醚受损的斑马鱼的生育能力。；Tssk6 is essential for the regulation of male zebrafish fertility. / Tssk6 对于调节雄性斑马鱼的生育能力至关重要。
- 产卵：67 篇；Acute administration of Manning compound during the spawning period reduces reproductive success in female zebrafish / 产卵期急性给药曼宁化合物会降低雌性斑马鱼的繁殖成功率；Exposure to Estrone Impairs Oogenesis and Spawning in Zebrafish (Danio rerio). / 暴露于雌激素会损害斑马鱼（Danio rerio）的卵生成和产卵。；孕激素去氢孕酮影响鱼类产卵过程的机制研究；Anti-Obesogenic and Antioxidant Potential of Lychee Seed Flour in Zebrafish Fed a High-Fat Diet. / 斑马鱼中荔枝种子粉具有抗产卵和抗氧化潜力，饲料为高脂饮食。；Decreased Viability and Changes in Sex Ratio in the Offspring of Danio rerio as a Result of Exposure to 2,2',5,5'-tetrachlorobiphenyl (PCB 52) on Producers during the Prespawning Period / 由于产卵前期对生产者暴露于2,2'，5,5'-四氯二苯（PCB 52），后代存活率下降及性别比变化
- 配子质量：7 篇；Identification of effective plant-based oils for use in aquafeed: An evaluation of impact on gamete quality and developmental success using zebrafish (Danio rerio) as a screening organism / 用于水饲的有效植物基油鉴定：利用斑马鱼（Danio rerio）作为筛选生物对配子质量和发育成功的影响评估；Correction to: 'Fasting increases investment in soma upon refeeding at the cost of gamete quality in zebrafish' (2023) by Ivimey-Cook et al. / 更正：Ivimey-Cook等人2023年提出的“禁食增加斑马鱼配子质量的再喂养时对体质的投资”。；Fasting increases investment in soma upon refeeding at the cost of gamete quality in zebrafish. / 禁食会增加斑马鱼配子质量的补食，但会降低体质。；Drugs and Therapies - Toxicology and Applied Pharmacology; Study Data from Urmia University Provide New Insights into Toxicology and Applied Pharmacology (Imatinib mesylate effects on zebrafish reproductive success: Gonadal development, gamete quality, fertility, embryo-larvae viability and ...) / 药物与疗法——毒理学与应用药理学;乌尔米耶大学的研究数据为毒理学和应用药理学提供了新的见解（伊马替尼甲基酸盐对斑马鱼繁殖成功的影响：性腺发育、配子质量、生育力、胚胎-幼体存活率等）；Imatinib mesylate effects on zebrafish reproductive success: Gonadal development, gamete quality, fertility, embryo-larvae viability and development, and related genes / 伊马替尼甲苯甲酸盐对斑马鱼繁殖成功的影响：性腺发育、配子质量、生育力、胚胎幼体存活率与发育及相关基因

- 母源效应：7 篇；Transposon insertion causes ctnnb2 transcript instability that results in the maternal effect zebrafish ichabod ( ich ) mutation. / 转座子插入会导致ctnnb2转录本不稳定性，进而引发母体效应斑马鱼（ich）突变。；Transposon insertion causes ctnnb2 transcript instability that results in the maternal effect zebrafish ichabod (ich) mutation. / 转座子插入会导致ctnnb2转录本不稳定，进而导致母体效应斑马鱼斑马鱼（ich）突变。；Heritable CRISPR Mutagenesis of Essential Maternal Effect Genes as a Simple Tool for Sustained Population Suppression of Invasive Species in a Zebrafish Model. / 遗传CRISPR诱变本质母体效应基因，作为斑马鱼模型中持续抑制入侵物种种群的简单工具。；Isolation and genetic characterization of mother-of-snow-white, a maternal effect allele affecting laterality and lateralized behaviors in zebrafish. / 雪白鱼母的分离与遗传特征，这是一种影响斑马鱼侧向性和侧化行为的母体效应等位基因。；A dorsalized and cell migration maternal effect mutant in Zebrafish / 斑马鱼中背化和细胞迁移的母体效应突变体
- 父源效应：0 篇；暂无对应论文

- 衰老：253 篇；大鲵活性肽对H2O2诱导斑马鱼衰老的保护作用；西藏桑黄多酚类化合物延缓衰老的网络药理学分析及体内外实验验证；薰衣草多糖对AD模型斑马鱼行为学及衰老小鼠生化病理指标的影响；A novel dual-pathway AhR mechanism underpins cardiac senescence induced by 6PPDQ. / 一种新型双通路AhR机制支撑着6PPDQ诱导的心脏衰老。；Adverse outcome pathway-based analysis of vanillic acid-induced neurodevelopmental toxicity and cellular senescence in zebrafish. / 基于不良结局的斑马鱼神经发育毒性和细胞衰老分析，由香尼拉酸诱导的神经发育毒性。

- 细胞衰老：63 篇；A novel dual-pathway AhR mechanism underpins cardiac senescence induced by 6PPDQ. / 一种新型双通路AhR机制支撑着6PPDQ诱导的心脏衰老。；Adverse outcome pathway-based analysis of vanillic acid-induced neurodevelopmental toxicity and cellular senescence in zebrafish. / 基于不良结局的斑马鱼神经发育毒性和细胞衰老分析，由香尼拉酸诱导的神经发育毒性。；Benzo[a]pyrene induces non-alcoholic fatty liver disease by exacerbating hepatic senescence and disrupting gut-liver axis in zebrafish. / 苯二氮卓[a]芘通过加重肝脏衰老和破坏斑马鱼的肠肝轴来诱发非酒精性脂肪肝病。；Correction: Opposing p53 and mTOR/AKT promote an in vivo switch from apoptosis to senescence upon telomere shortening in zebrafish / 更正：对抗p53和mTOR/AKT促进斑马鱼端粒缩短时体内从凋亡转向衰老；Glutamine Depletion Induced Senescence-Associated β-Galactosidase Activity and Impaired Functional Properties of Ea.hy926 Endothelial Cells / 谷氨酰胺的耗竭诱导了与衰老相关的β-半乳糖苷酶活性，并损害了Ea.hy926内皮细胞的功能特性
- 虚弱：2 篇；Assessing frailty in aged zebrafish using a quick pseudo-frailty index. / 使用快速伪虚弱指数评估老斑马鱼的虚弱度。；Zebrafish as a Human Muscle Model for Studying Age-Dependent Sarcopenia and Frailty / 以斑马鱼作为研究年龄依赖性肌少症和虚弱的人体肌肉模型
- 年龄相关退化：1 篇；Prolonged Supplementation of Ozonated Sunflower Oil Bestows an Antiaging Effect, Improves Blood Lipid Profile and Spinal Deformities, and Protects Vital Organs of Zebrafish ( Danio rerio ) against Age-Related Degeneration: Two-Years Consumption Study / 长期补充臭氧化葵花籽油具有抗衰老效果，改善血脂和脊柱畸形，并保护斑马鱼（Danio rerio）的重要器官免受年龄相关退化：两年食用研究
- 抗衰老：23 篇；Bridging traditional knowledge and modern discovery: The role of zebrafish in anti-aging research of medicinal and edible traditional Chinese medicine. / 传统知识与现代发现的桥梁：斑马鱼在药用及食用中医抗衰老研究中的作用。；Development of Polyethylene Glycol-Modified Liposomes of Phenolic Glycoside 1 for Enhanced Anti-Aging Effect / 开发苯酸苷苷1的聚乙二醇修饰脂分子，以增强抗衰老效果；Astragaloside IV and ceramide IIIB co-modified ferulic acid ethosomes: a promising novel approach for skin anti-aging. / 黄芪苷IV和神经酰胺IIIB联合修饰的阿魏酸乙醇体：一种有前景的抗衰老新方法。；Development of a Short Telomere Zebrafish Model for Accelerated Aging Research and Antiaging Drug Screening. / 开发了用于加速衰老研究和抗衰老药物筛查的短端粒斑马鱼模型。；Establishment and application of a zebrafish model of Werner syndrome identifies sapanisertib as a potential antiaging drug. / 斑马鱼模型的建立和应用将萨潘尼塞汀识别为潜在的抗衰老药物。

### 心脏与血管再生

- 心脏再生：248 篇；ankrd1a is consistently upregulated in cardiomyocytes bordering the injury or scar area and affects their dedifferentiation during zebrafish heart regeneration. / ankrd1a在受伤或瘢痕区域边缘的心肌细胞中持续上调，影响斑马鱼心脏再生过程中的去分化。；Attenuation of epicardial activation and myofibroblast abundance via the Fbln2–Nupr1b axis stimulates cardiac regeneration in zebrafish / 通过Fbln2–Nupr1b轴，心外膜激活和肌纤维母细胞丰度的减弱，刺激斑马鱼的心脏再生；Comparison of cardiac regeneration capacity between zebrafish and medaka reveals a regenerative response in both teleost species. / 比较斑马鱼与美鹬鱼的心脏再生能力，显示两种硬骨鱼均存在再生反应。；Fibrin defines tissue stiffness and biomechanical signaling in regenerating zebrafish hearts as revealed by high-resolution stiffness mapping / 纤维蛋白通过高分辨率刚度映射揭示了斑马鱼心脏再生中的组织刚性和生物力学信号传递；Genes for 5'mRNA cap binding proteins, eif4ea and eif4eb, have alternative roles during heart regeneration. / 5'mRNA帽结合蛋白的基因eif4ea和eif4eb在心脏再生过程中具有替代作用。
- 心肌再生：8 篇；甲羟戊酸二磷酸脱羧酶a调控斑马鱼心肌再生；Mongolian medicine Eerdun-Wurile promotes myocardial regeneration by regulating MVDA in zebrafish. / 蒙古医学Eerdun-Wurile通过调控斑马鱼的MVDA促进心肌再生。；心肌再生相关分子的调控机制；Lamin B2 Levels Regulate Polyploidization of Cardiomyocyte Nuclei and Myocardial Regeneration / 层联动蛋白B2水平调控心肌细胞核的多倍化和心肌再生；Adult sox10 + Cardiomyocytes Contribute to Myocardial Regeneration in the Zebrafish / 成年sox10心肌细胞参与斑马鱼的心肌再生
- 心肌细胞增殖：53 篇；Mechanisms and Therapeutic Potential of Human Cardiomyocyte Proliferation. / 人类心肌细胞增殖的机制与治疗潜力。；Novel Wnt potentiators induce iPSC-derived cardiomyocyte proliferation and increase embryonic zebrafish heart size. / 新型Wnt增强剂诱导iPSC来源心肌细胞增殖，并增加胚胎斑马鱼心脏大小。；Overexpression of Gopc Promotes Cardiomyocyte Proliferation but Delays Overall Heart Regeneration in Zebrafish. / Gopc的过度表达促进心肌细胞增殖，但会延迟斑马鱼的整体心脏再生。；Smarce1-dependent modulation of Stat3 signaling governs cardiomyocyte proliferation. / Smarce1依赖的Stat3信号调节控制心肌细胞增殖。；MORN4缺失通过调控心肌细胞增殖和凋亡影响斑马鱼心脏发育
- 瘢痕消退：1 篇；Collagenolytic Activity Is Associated with Scar Resolution in Zebrafish Hearts after Cryoinjury / 胶原蛋白溶解活性与斑马鱼冷冻损伤后心脏瘢痕的消退有关
- 再血管化：5 篇；Coronary Revascularization During Heart Regeneration Is Regulated by Epicardial and Endocardial Cues and Forms a Scaffold for Cardiomyocyte Repopulation. / 心脏再生期间的冠状动脉血运再生受心外膜和心内膜信号调控，形成心肌细胞再生的支架。；Neuropilin 1 mediates epicardial activation and revascularization in the regenerating zebrafish heart. / 神经毛囊1介导正在再生的斑马鱼心脏中的心外膜激活和血管再生。；Fast revascularization of the injured area is essential to support zebrafish heart regeneration. / 受伤部位的快速血运再生对于支持斑马鱼心脏再生至关重要。；Science; Studies from Max-Planck-Institute Reveal New Findings on Science (Fast revascularization of the injured area is essential to support zebrafish heart regeneration) / 科学;马克斯·普朗克研究所的研究揭示了科学新发现（受伤部位的快速血运恢复对支持斑马鱼心脏再生至关重要）；Regeneration of breeding tubercles on zebrafish pectoral fins requires androgens and two waves of revascularization. / 斑马鱼胸鳍繁殖结节的再生需要雄激素和两波血运再生。
- 血管再生：12 篇；Zebrafish radial glia orchestrate vascular regeneration: implications for bionic therapy of spinal cord injury. / 斑马鱼桡神经胶质细胞协调血管再生：对脊髓损伤仿生治疗的启示。；文冠果油促进斑马鱼血管再生的分子机制研究；基于线粒体MCU通道探究甲基莲心碱促血管再生抗脑缺血的作用机制；Apela promotes blood vessel regeneration and remodeling in zebrafish. / Apela促进斑马鱼的血管再生和重塑。；Collagen fibers provide guidance cues for capillary regrowth during regenerative angiogenesis in zebrafish. / 胶原纤维为斑马鱼再生血管生成过程中毛细血管再生提供指导线索。

- 神经再生：75 篇；Zebrafish neural regeneration: mechanistic insights into human nervous system repair. / 斑马鱼神经再生：人类神经系统修复的机制性见解。；Axonal defasciculation is restricted to specific branching points during regeneration of the lateral line nerve in zebrafish. / 斑马鱼侧线神经再生过程中，轴突脱壳仅限于特定的分支点。；Axonal defasciculation is restricted to specific branching points during regeneration of the lateral line nerve in zebrafish. / 斑马鱼侧线神经再生过程中，轴突脱壳仅限于特定的分支点。；Optimized in vivo two-photon imaging reveals the essential role of the contralateral eye in functional optic nerve regeneration in zebrafish larvae / 优化的体内双光子成像揭示了对侧眼在斑马鱼幼体功能性视神经再生中的关键作用；Serotonin neuromodulation directs optic nerve regeneration. / 血清素神经调节指导视神经再生。
- 胶质桥：1 篇；Central Nervous System; Studies from Duke University Have Provided New Data on Central Nervous System (Injury-induced ctgfa directs glial bridging and spinal cord regeneration in zebrafish) / 中枢神经系统;杜克大学的研究提供了关于中枢神经系统的新数据（损伤诱导的ctgfa指导斑马鱼胶质桥和脊髓再生）
- 脑修复：7 篇；Microglial depletion after brain injury prolongs inflammation and impairs brain repair, adult neurogenesis and pro-regenerative signaling. / 脑损伤后的微胶质细胞耗竭会延长炎症，损害大脑修复、成人神经生成和促进再生信号。；地塞米松介导肠道炎症及肠黏膜屏障功能对斑马鱼脑损伤修复的抑制作用；Distribution of microglia/immune cells in the brain of adult zebrafish in homeostatic and regenerative conditions: Focus on oxidative stress during brain repair. / 成年斑马鱼大脑中小胶质细胞/免疫细胞在稳态和再生条件下的分布：重点关注脑修复过程中的氧化应激。；Cellular Mechanisms Participating in Brain Repair of Adult Zebrafish and Mammals after Injury / 参与成年斑马鱼和哺乳动物受伤后大脑修复的细胞机制；Expression of adiponectin receptors in the brain of adult zebrafish and mouse: Links with neurogenic niches and brain repair. / 成年斑马鱼和小鼠大脑中脂联素受体的表达：与神经源生态位及脑修复的联系。
- 周围神经再生：5 篇；Probing cellular and molecular mechanisms of peripheral nerve regeneration using zebrafish / 利用斑马鱼探究周围神经再生的细胞和分子机制；A small molecule screen identifies in vivo modulators of peripheral nerve regeneration in zebrafish. / 小分子筛选法鉴定出斑马鱼体内调节周围神经再生的因子。；Biomedical Engineering - Tissue Engineering; Findings on Tissue Engineering Described by Researchers at University of Pennsylvania (A small molecule screen identifies in vivo modulators of peripheral nerve regeneration in zebrafish) / 生物医学工程——组织工程;宾夕法尼亚大学研究人员关于组织工程的发现（小分子筛选识别出斑马鱼体内调节周围神经再生的因子）；The lh3 Glycosyltransferase Directs Target-Selective Peripheral Nerve Regeneration / LH3糖基转移酶指导靶向选择性周围神经再生；Axon-Schwann cell interactions during peripheral nerve regeneration in zebrafish larvae. / 斑马鱼幼虫周围神经再生过程中的轴突-施旺细胞相互作用。

- 视网膜再生：93 篇；Brain-formed estrogen promotes retinal regeneration in zebrafish via nuclear estrogen receptor-dependent genomic signaling. / 脑中生成的雌激素通过核雌激素受体依赖的基因组信号促进斑马鱼的视网膜再生。；Glucose Exposure Suppresses Brain Aromatase and Impairs Retinal Regeneration in Zebrafish. / 葡萄糖暴露会抑制斑马鱼的脑芳香化酶，并损害视网膜再生。；Mesenchymal-derived neural progenitors underlie local insulin production and neuronal transdifferentiation during retina regeneration. / 间充质来源的神经前体在视网膜再生过程中，是局部胰岛素产生和神经元转分化的基础。；Will I see you again? The promise, power, and problems of retinal regeneration in fish. / 我还会再见到你吗？鱼类视网膜再生的承诺、力量与问题。；Analysis of the senescence secretome during zebrafish retina regeneration / 斑马鱼视网膜再生过程中衰老分泌组分析
- Muller胶质细胞：17 篇；NF-kB signaling negatively regulates Muller glia proliferation in the zebrafish retina / NF-kB信号负向调控斑马鱼视网膜中的穆勒胶质细胞增殖；Muller glia-supported optic nerve regeneration in the adult zebrafish is triggered by infiltrating macrophages or microglia depletion / 成年斑马鱼的穆勒胶质支持视神经再生是由浸润巨噬细胞或微胶质细胞枯竭触发的；Muller glial cell reprogramming and retina regeneration in zebrafish / 斑马鱼的穆勒胶质细胞重编程与视网膜再生；Histone Deacetylase-Mediated Muller Glia Reprogramming Through Her4.1-Lin28a axis is Essential for Retina Regeneration in Zebrafish / 通过Her4.1-Lin28a轴的组蛋白脱乙酰化酶介导穆勒胶质重编程对斑马鱼视网膜再生至关重要；Opposing Actions of Fgf8a on Notch Signaling Distinguish Two Muller Glial Cell Populations that Contribute to Retina Growth and Regeneration / fgf8a对切迹信号的相反作用区分了两种参与视网膜生长和再生的穆勒胶质细胞群体

- 视网膜损伤：13 篇；Visual neurotoxicity of zinc oxide nanoparticles on zebrafish larvae: Roles of retinal injury, redox imbalance, and impaired phototransduction. / 斑马鱼幼体氧化锌纳米颗粒的视觉神经毒性：视网膜损伤、氧化还原失衡和光传导受损的作用。；Photoreceptor regeneration occurs normally in microglia-deficient irf8 mutant zebrafish following acute retinal damage / 在急性视网膜损伤后，小胶质缺乏的irf8突变斑马鱼中，感光细胞再生正常发生；视网膜内源性多肽CKASKGKL调节p53/cyclinD1信号通路拮抗多氯联苯暴露致子代视网膜损伤；Inflammation modulates regeneration following acute or chronic retinal damage in zebrafish / 炎症调节斑马鱼急性或慢性视网膜损伤后的再生；Recapitulation of Retinal Damage in Zebrafish Larvae Infected with Zika Virus / 斑马鱼幼体感染兹卡病毒视网膜损伤的重现
- 视神经再生：44 篇；Optimized in vivo two-photon imaging reveals the essential role of the contralateral eye in functional optic nerve regeneration in zebrafish larvae / 优化的体内双光子成像揭示了对侧眼在斑马鱼幼体功能性视神经再生中的关键作用；Serotonin neuromodulation directs optic nerve regeneration. / 血清素神经调节指导视神经再生。；The transcription factor Jun is necessary for optic nerve regeneration in larval zebrafish. / 转录因子Jun是斑马鱼幼体视神经再生所必需的。；非侵入式频闪光促进斑马鱼视神经再生的作用和机制研究；Noninvasive Light Flicker Stimulation Promotes Optic Nerve Regeneration by Activating Microglia and Enhancing Neural Plasticity in Zebrafish. / 非侵入性光闪烁刺激通过激活小胶质细胞并增强斑马鱼的神经可塑性，促进视神经再生。

- 鳍再生：213 篇；2,6-Dibromophenol inhibits fin regeneration in zebrafish larvae via Wnt-mediated immune cell recruitment. / 2,6-二溴酚通过Wnt介导的免疫细胞招募，抑制斑马鱼幼虫的鳍再生。；二丙酸倍氯米松对斑马鱼幼鱼尾鳍再生的影响；Decaying and expanding Erk gradients process memory of skeletal size during zebrafish fin regeneration / 衰变和扩展的Erk梯度在斑马鱼鳍再生过程中处理骨骼大小的记忆；Extract from crown-of-thorns starfish promotes zebrafish caudal fin regeneration by inhibition of neutrophil migration. / 冠棘海星提取物通过抑制中性粒细胞迁移促进斑马鱼尾鳍再生。；Heme oxygenase 1 activity mediates red blood cell clearance and tail fin regeneration in zebrafish larvae. / 血红素加氧酶1活性介导斑马鱼幼虫的红细胞清除和尾鳍再生。
