### 抗炎、抗氧化与组织保护

- 抗炎作用：207 篇；冠心静及其单体藁本内酯抑制巨噬细胞活化与抗炎作用；黄花败酱草不同极性部位的抗炎活性筛选及作用机制研究；基于UPLC-MS、网络药理学和斑马鱼模型的白蓼花抗炎机制和活性成分研究；荆银颗粒物质基础及其抗炎活性研究；救必应抗炎活性评价及抗炎机制初探
- 抗氧化作用：191 篇；百合复合发酵液抗氧化能力及对斑马鱼尾鳍修复功效的研究；猴头菌发酵山楂过程中活性成分及体内外抗氧化活性的变化；萝卜膳食纤维-植物乳植杆菌共包埋微胶囊的构建及其在斑马鱼肠道定植与抗氧化活性研究；阳江山药非淀粉多糖制备及其抗氧化活性研究；中亚白及酸性多糖结构解析及抗氧化活性研究
- 抗糖化：9 篇；白簕提取物基于斑马鱼模型及体外糖化模型的抗炎、抗氧化及抗糖化活性研究；Mixtures of EGCG, bamboo leaf flavonoids, and broccoli seed water extracts exhibit anti-glycation and skin-protective effects / EGCG、竹叶类黄酮和西兰花种子水提取物的混合物具有抗糖化和皮肤保护作用；波森莓的抗糖化与美白效果评价研究；Cuban Policosanol (Raydel®) Exerts Higher Antioxidant and Anti-Glycation Activities than Chinese Policosanol (BOC Sciences) in Reconstituted High-Density Lipoproteins: In Vivo Anti-Inflammatory Activities in Zebrafish and Its Embryos. / 古巴聚糖醇（雷德®尔）在重组高密度脂蛋白中发挥的抗氧化和抗糖基化活性高于中国聚糖醇（BOC Sciences）：斑马鱼及其胚胎体内抗炎活性。；Synergistic Anti-Inflammatory Activity of Lipid-Free Apolipoprotein (apo) A-I and CIGB-258 in Acute-Phase Zebrafish via Stabilization of the apoA-I Structure to Enhance Anti-Glycation and Antioxidant Activities / 通过稳定apoA-I结构，增强抗糖化和抗氧化活性，使无脂载脂蛋白A-I和CIGB-258在急性期斑马鱼中发挥协同抗炎活性
- 抗过敏：2 篇；发酵粘液乳杆菌HCS08-005冻干粉制备工艺优化及其抗过敏功能；库拉索芦荟花抗过敏功效研究
- 抗应激：10 篇；Pmpcb modulates zebrafish neurogenesis and stress resistance via regulating mitochondria metabolism and function / PMPCB通过调节线粒体的代谢和功能，调节斑马鱼的神经发生和抗应激能力；酵母钴的制备、表征及其在鱼抗应激中的应用；Stress-resilient effect of Spirulina platensis on zebrafish chronic unpredictable stress model. / 螺旋藻对斑马鱼慢性不可预测应激模型的抗应激韧性效应。；Behavioral and cortisol analysis of the anti-stress effect of Polygonum minus (Huds) extracts in chronic unpredictable stress (CUS) zebrafish model / 慢性不可预测压力（CUS）斑马鱼模型中，Polygonum minus（Huds）提取物抗应激作用的行为和皮质醇分析；Anti-stress effects of the glucagon-like peptide-1 receptor agonist liraglutide in zebrafish. / 斑马鱼中胰高血糖素样肽-1受体激动剂利拉格鲁肽的抗应激作用。
- 减毒作用：6 篇；废弃菌棒资源化利用及其减毒效应研究；基于斑马鱼幼鱼的补骨脂盐炙减毒存效探索；补骨脂雷公炮制过程质量传递与减毒存效科学内涵探讨；甘遂代表性二萜类成分炮制转化及减毒存效作用研究；基于中药化学生物学四逆汤组方配伍减毒作用研究
- 解毒作用：58 篇；Chemical pollution alters detoxification capacity and behavior in the native freshwater fish Galaxias maculatus under in situ exposure. / 化学污染会改变本地淡水鱼Galaxias maculatus在原位暴露下的解毒能力和行为。；Efficient biodegradation and detoxification of 6:2 fluorotelomer sulfonic acid by Phanerochaete chrysosporium: Insights into enzymatic mechanisms and reduced ecotoxicity / 6：2氟端体磺酸的高效生物降解与解毒：揭示酶机制及降低生态毒性；Enhanced detoxification of benzotriazole during catalytic ozonation via interfacial interactions / 通过界面相互作用促进苯三唑催化臭氧作用的解毒增强；Genome-Guided Discovery of Enterobacter Multicopper Oxidases Enabling Enzymatic Detoxification of Aflatoxin B1. / 基因组引导发现了肠道杆菌多铜氧化酶，使黄曲霉毒素B1能够酶解。；MneA and TerC exhibit distinct roles in manganese detoxification and virulence in Vibrio parahaemolyticus. / MneA和TerC在副溶血弧菌中对锰的解毒和致病性具有不同作用。
- 组织保护：2 篇；Evaluation of anti-hyperglycemic, hepatic gene-modulatory and tissue-protective effects of curcumin, curcumin-loaded zeolitic imidazolate framework-8, and metformin in zebrafish (Danio rerio) / 评估斑马鱼（Danio rerio）中姜黄素、含姜黄素的沸素含吡唑酸框架-8和二甲双胍的抗高血糖、肝脏基因调控及组织保护作用；Co-Supplementation of Policosanol and Banaba Leaf Extract Exhibited a Cooperative Effect Against Hyperglycemia and Dyslipidemia in Zebrafish: Highlighting Vital Organ Protection Against High-Cholesterol and High-Galactose Diet / 聚氯醇和巴纳巴叶提取物的联合补充显示出对斑马鱼高血糖和血脂异常的协同效应：突出对高胆固醇和高半乳糖饮食的重要器官保护
- 损伤缓解：0 篇；暂无对应论文

### 重金属与无机污染物

- 铅暴露：262 篇；不同粒径微塑料对铅在斑马鱼体内富集和氧化应激的影响；Chronic nephrotoxicity and Nrf2-mediated oxidative stress response in zebrafish (Danio rerio) kidneys following environmentally relevant co-exposure to lead and chromium. / 斑马鱼（Danio rerio）肾脏在与环境相关铅和铬共暴露后，出现慢性肾毒性和Nrf2介导的氧化应激反应。；Co-exposure to lead and copper induces ferroptosis-related neurotoxicity in zebrafish larvae via oxidative stress and mitochondrial dysfunction. / 铅和铜的同时暴露会通过氧化应激和线粒体功能障碍诱发斑马鱼幼体的铁消亡相关神经毒性。；Combined Aluminum Chloride and Lead Acetate Exposure Induces Anxiety-like Behavior, Cognitive Impairment, and Cholinergic–Oxidative Dysregulation in Adult Zebrafish: Optimization of a Dementia-like Model / 氯化铝和醋酸铅联合暴露诱发成年斑马鱼的焦虑样行为、认知障碍及胆碱能-氧化失调：痴呆样模型的优化；Combined developmental toxicity of organophosphorus flame retardant TCEP and lead on zebrafish / 有机磷阻燃剂TCEP与铅对斑马鱼的联合发育毒性
- 镉暴露：417 篇；“Biochemical impacts of polyethylene terephthalate microplastics and cadmium on Danio rerio under environmental conditions” / “聚对苯二甲酸乙二醇酯微塑料和镉在环境条件下对丹尼奥·雷里奥的生化影响”；腐殖酸和硫化镉纳米颗粒复合暴露对斑马鱼的神经发育毒性研究；Acidification, warming, and cadmium exposure disrupt zebrafish calcium metabolism, bone formation and swimming activity. / 酸化、变暖和镉暴露会干扰斑马鱼钙的代谢、骨骼形成和游泳活动。；Cadmium-induced disruption of monoaminergic signaling and motor behavior in developing zebrafish / 镉诱导的单胺能信号传导和发育斑马鱼运动行为的破坏；Combating Cadmium-Induced Neurotoxicity, Oxidative Stress, and Inflammatory Pathways Using DOPA-31, a Dioxopiperidinamide Derivative in an In Vivo Zebrafish Model. / 利用DOPA-31（一种二氧哌啶酰胺衍生物）在体内斑马鱼模型中对抗镉诱导的神经毒性、氧化应激和炎症通路。
- 汞暴露：214 篇；A highly selective fluorescent probe for visualizing hazardous mercury ions in living cells and zebrafish. / 一种高度选择性的荧光探针，用于观察活细胞和斑马鱼中的有害汞离子。；Balancing sensitivity and environmental safety in fluorescent probe design: improved ethanol-thermal carbon dots enable a leap in mercury(II) detection and zebrafish-assessed safety. / 在荧光探针设计中平衡灵敏度与环境安全：改进的乙醇-热碳点使汞（II）检测和斑马鱼安全性评估实现了飞跃。；Embryonic exposure to mercury contamination is associated with elevated corticosterone and lower body condition in wild Smooth-fronted caiman hatchlings. / 胚胎阶段接触汞污染与野生光滑额凯门鳄幼龟皮质酮升高和下半身状况相关。；Phenylmercury induces structural and functional alterations on hemoglobin: From isolated protein to a biological model. / 苯汞诱导血红蛋白的结构和功能改变：从分离蛋白到生物模型。；ZEBRAFISH PARENTAL CO-EXPOSURE TO ENVIRONMENTALLY RELEVANT LEVELS OF INORGANIC ARSENIC + METHYLMERCURY IMPACTS NEUROBEHAVIOR IN PARENTS AND PROGENY. / 斑马鱼亲代共暴露于环境相关水平的无机砷甲基汞，会影响亲代和后代的神经行为。

### 组学与系统生物学

- 转录组学：1006 篇；基于网络药理学、转录组学和动物实验探讨葛根素对糖尿病肾脏损伤的影响；基于形态学与转录组学分析：石斛碱缓解斑马鱼糖尿病视网膜病变；基于CYP抑制剂和转录组学研究杠柳毒苷通过抑制HNF4α-CYP轴诱导斑马鱼肝损伤；基于RNA-seq和ChIP-seq解析Stat5b调控斑马鱼生长性别二态性的机制；A core innate immune gene panel derived from germ-free larval zebrafish transcriptomics resolves strain-specific induction by Aeromonas veronii and Lacticaseibacillus rhamnosus GG. / 一个核心先天免疫基因面板，源自无菌斑马鱼幼体转录组学，解析了Aeromonas veronii和Lacticaseibacillus rhamnosus GG对菌株特异性诱导的问题。
- 单细胞转录组：97 篇；A single cell transcriptomics-based analysis provides mechanistic insights into the chronic low-concentration effects on hematopoietic disruption in zebrafish(Danio rerio) by Antinomy. / 基于单细胞转录组学的分析为斑马鱼（Danio rerio）中对造血破坏的慢性低浓度效应提供了机制性见解。；Anxiety-like behavior and social stress in adult zebrafish induced by 6PPD: insights from single-cell RNA sequencing. / 6PPD诱导的成年斑马鱼焦虑样行为和社交压力：单细胞RNA测序的见解。；Comparative single-cell transcriptomics of aquatic and terrestrial model vertebrate olfactory epithelium / 水生与陆生模型脊椎动物嗅上皮的比较单细胞转录组学；Generation of a prop1 knockin zebrafish enables single-cell transcriptomics of early pituitary development. / 生成prop1敲击斑马鱼使得早期垂体发育的单细胞转录组学成为可能。；Genome-wide identification and single-cell transcriptomic analysis of the mcm gene family in zebrafish reveals its role in TCDD-induced reproductive toxicity. / 斑马鱼mcm基因家族的全基因组鉴定和单细胞转录组分析揭示了其在TCDD诱导生殖毒性中的作用。
- 空间转录组：7 篇；Zero-shot reconstruction of mutant spatial transcriptomes / 突变体空间转录组的零样本重建；Spatial transcriptomic imaging of an intact organism using volumetric DNA microscopy / 利用体积DNA显微镜对完整生物进行空间转录组成像；Spatially Compact Arrangement of Larval Zebrafish Sections for Spatial Transcriptomic Analysis. / 《用于空间转录组分析的斑马鱼幼体切片空间紧凑排列》。；3D可视化重构技术在斑马鱼咽囊期空间转录组图谱绘制中的应用；Spatial transcriptomic identification of regional responses and ligand-receptor interactions during optic nerve regeneration in zebrafish / 斑马鱼视神经再生过程中区域反应及配体-受体相互作用的空间转录组识别
- 代谢组学：327 篇；基于非靶向代谢组学、网络药理学和斑马鱼模型研究南阳月季花的抗氧化作用机制；基于肝脏代谢组学的开心散抗抑郁作用机制初探；A metabolomics study unravels the hepatotoxic mechanism of diuron in zebrafish: Disruption of glutathione synthesis and mitochondrial energy metabolism. / 一项代谢组学研究揭示了斑马鱼二铀龙的肝毒性机制：谷胱甘肽合成的破坏和线粒体能量代谢。；CuO Nanoparticles Trigger Cuproptosis-Linked Mitochondrial Damage and Gut Microbiota-Metabolome Disruption in Zebrafish. / CuO纳米颗粒会触发斑马鱼中与蜂巢管菌相关线粒体损伤和肠道微生物群-代谢组的破坏。；Disruption of steroidogenesis and calcium signaling drives VX-induced reproductive toxicity in zebrafish: A spatial metabolomic and transcriptomic study. / 类固醇生成和钙信号传导的破坏驱动斑马鱼的VX诱导生殖毒性：一项空间代谢组学和转录组学研究。
- 蛋白质组学：252 篇；[Investigation on antidepressant mechanism of Piper laetispicum extract based on quantitative proteomics and zebrafish model of depression induced by chronic unpredictable mild stress]. / [基于定量蛋白质组学和斑马鱼模型，探讨由慢性不可预测轻度压力诱发抑郁症的抗抑郁机制研究]。；基于定量蛋白质组学技术与慢性不可预知温和刺激斑马鱼抑郁模型探讨大叶蒟提取物抗抑郁的作用机制；Dichloromethane Extract Is Responsible for the Evodiae Fructus induced Hepatotoxicity via PI3K-AKT/MAPK/p53/NF-κB Signaling Pathways through Integrated Network Pharmacology, Proteomic and Transcriptomic Analyses. / 二氯甲烷提取物通过综合网络药理学、蛋白质组学和转录组分析，通过PI3K-AKT/MAPK/p53/NF-κB信号通路，负责Evodiae fructus诱导的肝毒性。；Integrative proteomics and metabolomics approach to elucidate the multi-organ developmental toxicity of decabromodiphenyl ethane in zebrafish larvae / 整合蛋白质组学与代谢组学方法阐明斑马鱼幼体中十溴二苯乙烷的多器官发育毒性；Proteome-wide reverse molecular docking reveals folate receptor as a mediator of PFAS-induced neurodevelopmental toxicity. / 全蛋白质组的逆向分子结合揭示了叶酸受体是PFAS诱导神经发育毒性的介导剂。

- 基因组学：357 篇；Antiepileptic potential of salicylhydroxamic acid: genomic, molecular docking, and functional evaluations in pentylenetetrazole-induced adult zebrafish / 水杨酰羟胺酸的抗癫痫潜力：戊二四唑诱导成年斑马鱼的基因组学、分子结合及功能评估；Functional genomics analysis of developing zebrafish and human endoderm reveals highly conserved cis-regulatory modules acting during vertebrate organogenesis. / 对发育中的斑马鱼和人类内胚层的功能基因组学分析显示，在脊椎动物器官发生过程中，这些顺式调控模块高度保守。；Functional Genomics Through Zebrafish CRISPR Prioritizes Candidate Genes for Hemifacial Microsomia / 通过斑马鱼CRISPR功能基因组学优先选择半面小肌症候选基因；Genome-wide identification and single-cell transcriptomic analysis of the mcm gene family in zebrafish reveals its role in TCDD-induced reproductive toxicity. / 斑马鱼mcm基因家族的全基因组鉴定和单细胞转录组分析揭示了其在TCDD诱导生殖毒性中的作用。；Hexafluoropropylene oxide trimer acid (HFPO-TA) exposure predisposes to MASLD through reprogramming hepatic epigenome and transcriptome / 六氟丙二三三酸（HFPO-TA）暴露通过重编程肝表全基因组和转录组，使MASLD易发生
- 表观组学：5 篇；基于RNA-seq和ChIP-seq解析Stat5b调控斑马鱼生长性别二态性的机制；Epigenomics and transcriptomics profiles of developing zebrafish heart cells / 发育中的斑马鱼心脏细胞的表观基因组学和转录组学谱；一个Chip-seq的生物信息分析流程；The toxicological application of transcriptomics and epigenomics in zebrafish and other teleosts. / 转录组学和表观基因组学在斑马鱼及其他硬骨鱼中的毒理学应用。；The developmental epigenomics toolbox: ChIP-seq and MethylCap-seq profiling of early zebrafish embryos / 发育表观基因组学工具箱：早期斑马鱼胚胎的ChIP-seq和MethylCap-seq分析

- 染色质重塑：22 篇；From two to one: spatiotemporal mapping of chromatin remodelling in the protamine-lacking early zygote of zebrafish. / 从二到一：斑马鱼缺乏蛋白的早期合子染色质重塑的时空定位。；MORF4L1, encoding a chromatin remodeler, is mutated in a recognizable dysmorphic neurodevelopmental disorder. / 编码染色质重塑剂的MORF4L1，在一种可识别的变形神经发育障碍中发生突变。；Epigenetic regulation of cilia stability and kidney development by the chromatin remodeling SWI/SNF complex in zebrafish. / 斑马鱼染色质重塑SWI/SNF复合物对纤毛稳定性和肾脏发育的表观遗传调控。；Nap1l4a介导的染色质重塑调控斑马鱼的原始红细胞生成；In Vivo Genetic Screen Identifies Chromatin Remodeling Gene SET As a Collaborating Oncogenic Driver in Initiation and Maintenance of Acute Lymphoblastic Leukemia / 体内遗传筛选鉴定染色质重塑基因SET作为急性淋巴细胞白血病起始和维持的协同致癌驱动因素
- 微小RNA：344 篇；Acute and sub-chronic exposure to bisphenol AF induces cardiac inflammatory response in zebrafish through lncRNA/circRNA-miRNA-mRNA regulatory networks / 急性和亚慢性双酚AF暴露可通过lncRNA/circRNA-miRNA-mRNA调控网络诱导斑马鱼心脏炎症反应；microRNA-21 promotes dysregulated lipid metabolism and hepatocellular carcinoma. / 微小RNA-21促进脂质代谢失调和肝细胞癌。；MicroRNA-21-3p regulation of NADPH oxidase 4 and vascular endothelial growth factor A contributes to hemorrhage in cerebral cavernous malformations / 微小RNA-21-3p调控NADPH氧化酶4和血管内皮生长因子A，有助于脑海绵状血管畸形的出血；miRNA 183 Knockout Alters Cone Subtype Distribution, Transcriptional Activity and ERG Signals in the Tetrachromatic Zebrafish Visual System / miRNA 183 敲除变异体在四色斑马鱼视觉系统中的锥状细胞亚型分布、转录活性和ERG信号；Targeted blocking of gene splicing can dysregulate intron-embedded primary microRNAs. / 基因剪接的靶向阻断可以调节内含子嵌入的初级微小RNA。

### 信号通路与分子网络

- Wnt信号：224 篇；基于斑马鱼胚胎表型筛选具有抑制Wnt信号通路活性的海洋来源化合物；Cordycepin alleviates aluminium chloride-induced neurodevelopmental toxicity in zebrafish embryos by modulating inflammation and Wnt signaling. / 冬虫草通过调节炎症和Wnt信号，缓解斑马鱼胚胎中氯化铝引起的神经发育毒性。；Pilot: Wnt signaling controls pgam2 expression in zebrafish cardiomyocytes. / 飞行员：Wnt信号控制斑马鱼心肌细胞中的pgam2表达。；Reciprocal inhibition of Wnt signaling pathways pattern the interconnection of epithelial tubules in the regenerating zebrafish kidney. / Wnt信号通路的互惠抑制形成了再生斑马鱼肾脏上皮小管的相互连接。；Retraction notice to "Molecular structure of Fgfbp1 protein and its regulation of zebrafish cartilage development and homeostasis: Implications for Wnt signaling and ECM stability" [Int. - Notch信号：170 篇；Bmp16 Regulates Arterial Valve Morphogenesis Through Modulation of Notch Signaling in Zebrafish / Bmp16通过调节斑马鱼的切迹信号来调控动脉瓣形态发生；Camk2g1 Regulates Notch Signaling and Histone Methylation to Specify Hematopoietic Stem Cells. / Camk2g1调控切迹信号传导和组蛋白甲基化，以指定造血干细胞。；Gallic Acid Protects Against LPS-Induced Cardiac Dysfunction by Modulating Inflammatory Responses and Notch Signaling in Zebrafish Embryo Larvae. / 戊胃酸通过调节斑马鱼胚胎幼虫的炎症反应和切迹信号，保护其免受LPS诱发的心脏功能障碍。；miR-150 controls developmental angiogenesis via ribosome biogenesis-dependent regulation of Notch signaling. / miR-150通过核糖体生物发生依赖性调控切迹信号，控制发育血管生成。；Ochratoxin C induces zebrafish embryonic neurodevelopmental defects and cerebral hemorrhaging by downregulating Notch signaling / 赭曲毒素C通过下调Notch信号，诱导斑马鱼胚胎神经发育缺陷和脑出血
- Hedgehog信号：95 篇；Gli3R-mediated inhibition of hedgehog signaling alters the embryonic transcriptome in zebrafish. / Gli3R介导的刺猬信号抑制改变了斑马鱼的胚胎转录组。；Cannabidiol exerts teratogenic effects on developing zebrafish through the sonic hedgehog signaling pathway / 大麻二酚通过声波刺猬信号通路对发育中的斑马鱼产生致畸作用；Evolutionary History of Hedgehog Signaling Genes and Smoothened Dose Sensitivity in Teleosts / 刺猬信号基因的进化历史与硬骨鱼剂量敏感性平滑化；Gli3R-mediated inhibition of hedgehog signaling alters the embryonic transcriptome in zebrafish. / Gli3R介导的刺猬信号抑制改变了斑马鱼的胚胎转录组。；Sonic hedgehog signaling promotes basal epidermal fibrillin 3 expression for zebrafish fin ray branching. / 声波刺猬信号促进斑马鱼鳍射线分支的基底表皮纤维素3表达。
- BMP信号：117 篇；Graded BMP signals modulate yellow and red color in fishes, impacting adult pigment patterns and conspecific shoaling behavior. / 分级BMP信号调节鱼类的黄色和红色，影响成鱼的色素分布和同种群游行为。；Hedgehog and Bmp signaling pathways play opposing roles during establishment of the cardiac inflow tract in zebrafish. / 刺猬信号通路和Bmp信号通路在斑马鱼心脏流入通路建立过程中扮演相反角色。；Acvr2b receptors transduce all BMP signaling in the zebrafish gastrula and restrict Fibrodysplasia Ossificans Progressiva ACVR1-R206H signaling in a dose-dependent manner. / Acvr2b受体在斑马鱼胃管中转导所有BMP信号，并以剂量依赖性方式限制纤维异形成性骨化型（Progressiva ACVR1-R206H）信号。；Agouti and BMP signaling drive a naturally occurring fate conversion of melanophores to leucophores in zebrafish. / 斑马鱼中，黑素素和BMP信号传导驱动了一种自然发生的命运转化。；BMP signaling promotes zebrafish heart regeneration via alleviation of replication stress. / BMP信号通过减轻复制压力促进斑马鱼心脏再生。
- FGF信号：91 篇；An Inducible Zebrafish Model of Thrombocytopenia Enables In Vivo Drug Screening and Reveals FGF Signaling as a Key Regulator of Platelet Regeneration. / 诱导性斑马鱼血小板减少模型支持体内药物筛选，并揭示FGF信号作为血小板再生的关键调控因子。；Cooperation between Nodal and FGF signals regulates zebrafish cardiac cell migration and heart morphogenesis. / 节点与FGF信号的合作调控斑马鱼心脏细胞迁移和心脏形态发生。；Insights into Craniofacial Development and Anomalies: Exploring Fgf Signaling in Zebrafish Models. / 《颅面发育与异常的见解：探索斑马鱼模型中的FGF信号传导》。；nkx2.3 is responsible for posterior pharyngeal cartilage formation by inhibiting Fgf signaling / nkx2.3 通过抑制 Fgf 信号，负责后咽软骨的形成；Zbtb16 mediates a switch between Fgf signalling regimes in the developing hindbrain. / Zbtb16介导发育中后脑中Fgf信号传导模式之间的切换。
- mTOR信号：41 篇；Pro-myogenic and anti-atrophic effects of Padina arborescens extract through Akt/mTOR signaling in dexamethasone-induced C2C12 cells and zebrafish model / 通过Akt/mTOR信号提取的Padina arborescens在地塞米松诱导的C2C12细胞和斑马鱼模型中的肌源和抗萎缩作用；Agnuside enhances neuronal survival and cognitive function by modulating PI3K/AKT/mTOR signaling in Aluminium Chloride induced Alzheimer's disease. / 阿格努苷通过调节氯化铝诱发阿尔茨海默病中的PI3K/AKT/mTOR信号，提升神经元存活率和认知功能。；B-Type Trimeric Procyanidins Attenuate Nonalcoholic Hepatic Steatosis Through AMPK/mTOR Signaling Pathway in Oleic Acid-Induced HepG2 Cells and High-Fat Diet- Fed Zebrafish / B型三聚体蛋白酶通过AMPK/mTOR信号通路减弱油酸诱导的肝G2细胞和高脂饮食斑马鱼中的非酒精肝脂肪变；Caulerpa lentillifera extract alleviates the chronic diquat-induced liver oxidative damage in zebrafish (Danio rerio) in association with Nrf2 and AMPK/mTOR signaling pathway. / Caulerpa lentillifera 提取物通过 Nrf2 和 AMPK/mTOR 信号通路缓解斑马鱼（Danio rerio）慢性二水致导的肝脏氧化损伤。；EGCG attenuates BPA -induced male reproductive toxicity by regulating the blood-testis barrier by suppressing autophagy via AMPK/AKT/mTOR signaling pathway. / EGCG通过通过AMPK/AKT/mTOR信号通路抑制自噬，调节血睾屏障，从而减弱BPA诱导的男性生殖毒性。
- MAPK信号：43 篇；Inflammatory activation and MAPK signaling mediate fumonisin B1-induced myocardial dysfunction. / 炎症激活和MAPK信号介导由布莫尼辛B1诱导的心肌功能障碍。；Intermittent Administration Alleviates Enrofloxacin-Induced Hepatotoxicity in Zebrafish via Adaptive Lipid Remodeling and p38 MAPK Signaling / 间歇性给药通过适应性脂质重塑和p38 MAPK信号，缓解斑马鱼因恩诺氟沙星引起的肝毒性；The ezrin Gene Regulates Early Cardiac Morphogenesis and Contractile Function in Zebrafish Through the Coordinated Regulation of Apoptosis, Calcium Homeostasis, and the MAPK Signaling Pathway / ezrin基因通过协调调控细胞凋亡、钙稳态和MAPK信号通路，调控斑马鱼早期心脏形态发生和收缩功能；Trihexyphenidyl Ameliorates Depression-like Behaviors in Adult Zebrafish Exposed to Chronic Unpredictable Stress, Consistent with Regulation of the MAPK Signaling Pathway / 三己苯丙啶能缓解暴露于慢性不可预测压力下的成年斑马鱼的抑郁样行为，这与MAPK信号通路的调控一致；Co-Exposure of Microplastics and Avermectin at Environmental-Related Concentrations Caused Severe Heart Damage Through ROS-Mediated MAPK Signaling in Larval and Adult Zebrafish / 微塑料和阿维菌素在环境相关浓度下同时暴露，通过ROS介导的MAPK信号在幼鱼和成年斑马鱼中造成严重心脏损伤
- PI3K-AKT信号：7 篇；心可舒片调控TNF和PI3K-Akt信号通路改善斑马鱼高脂血症；Isoalantolactone protects against ethanol-induced gastric ulcer via alleviating inflammation through regulation of PI3K-Akt signaling pathway and Th17 cell differentiation. / 异兰内酯通过调控PI3K-Akt信号通路和Th17细胞分化缓解炎症，从而预防乙醇诱发的胃溃疡。；Mutations in TOP2B cause autosomal-dominant hereditary hearing loss via inhibition of the PI3K-Akt signalling pathway. / TOP2B突变通过抑制PI3K-Akt信号通路导致常染色体显性遗传性听力损失。；K-ras/PI3K-Akt signaling is essential for zebrafish hematopoiesis and angiogenesis. / K-ras/PI3K-Akt信号对斑马鱼造血和血管生成至关重要。；A VEGFR1 antagonistic peptide inhibits tumor growth and metastasis through VEGFR1-PI3K-AKT signaling pathway inhibition. / VEGFR1拮抗肽通过抑制VEGFR1-PI3K-AKT信号通路抑制肿瘤生长和转移。

### 细胞死亡、自噬与应激

- 细胞凋亡：657 篇；A novel tetrahydrocarbazole derivative-based lysosome-targeted fluorescent probe for the detection of apoptosis and polarity change in biological system. / 一种新型的四氢卡巴唑衍生物基溶酶体靶向荧光探针，用于检测细胞凋亡和生物系统极性变化。；Alpha-lipoic acid attenuates valproic acid-induced autism-like phenotypes in zebrafish by enhancing antioxidant capacity and repressing oxidative stress, inflammation, apoptosis, and HPI-axis signaling. / α-硫辛酸通过增强抗氧化能力并抑制氧化应激、炎症、凋亡和HPI轴信号，减弱斑马鱼中由丙戊酸诱导的自闭症样表型。；Antagonistic developmental cardiotoxicity of fluroxypyr-meptyl and carfentrazone-ethyl in zebrafish embryos via oxidative stress and apoptosis. / 斑马鱼胚胎中氟氧吡啶甲基和卡芬曲酮乙基通过氧化应激和凋亡引起的受阻性发育性心脏毒性。；BIRC3 (Encoding Cellular Inhibitor of Apoptosis Protein 2) Variants Result in Dysregulated Receptor-Interacting Protein Kinase 1 Signaling Leading to Increased Epithelial Cell Death and Are Associated With Monogenic Crohn's Disease. / BIRC3（编码细胞凋亡蛋白2）变异导致受体相互作用蛋白激酶1信号失调，导致上皮细胞死亡增加，并与单基因克罗恩病相关。；Carbapenem-resistant Klebsiella pneumoniae exacerbates virulence through oxidative stress, apoptosis induction and gut microbiota dysbiosis in zebrafish models. / 碳青霉烯耐药性肺炎克雷伯氏菌通过氧化应激、细胞凋亡诱导和斑马鱼模型中的肠道微生物群失调加剧毒力。
- 自噬：295 篇；cGAS attenuates hypoxia-inducible factor signaling via chaperone-mediated autophagic degradation of HIFα. / cGAS通过伴侣介导的HIFα自噬降解，减弱缺氧诱导因子信号传导。；Dihydropyrano[2,3-b]indoles: Novel dual modulators for lung cancer treatment via autophagy inhibition and ferroptosis induction. / 二氢吡喃[2,3-b]吲哚：通过自噬抑制和铁剥坏诱导治疗肺癌的新型双重调节剂。；Exposure to dimethyl phthalate induces oxidative stress and autophagy in gill of Zebrafish via the PI3K/AKT/mTOR pathway: Protective role of 1,8-cineole. / 暴露于邻苯二甲酸酯通过PI3K/AKT/mTOR途径诱导斑马鱼鳃的氧化应激和自噬：1,8-cinole的保护作用。；Glabridin promotes melanosome degradation and alleviates melanosome-induced mitochondrial dysfunction in keratinocytes via autophagy / 格拉布里丁通过自噬促进黑色素体降解，并缓解角质细胞中黑色素体诱导的线粒体功能障碍；Neuroprotective effects of idebenone in a zebrafish model of Parkinson's disease via regulating autophagy, mitigating apoptosis and oxidative stress. / 在斑马鱼帕金森病模型中，伊苯酮通过调节自噬、减轻凋亡和氧化应激发挥神经保护作用。
- 铁死亡：128 篇；壮骨止痛方抗骨质疏松大鼠铁死亡及抗斑马鱼铁过载作用；Alox12 Mediates the Temporal Shift of Arsenite-Induced Ferroptosis in Zebrafish Embryonic Fibroblast ZF4 Cells. / Alox12介导斑马鱼胚胎成纤维细胞ZF4细胞中砷矿诱导铁消亡的时间偏移。；American ginseng-derived extracellular vesicle-like nanoparticles (AGELNs) mitigate doxorubicin-induced cardiotoxicity by inhibiting GPX4-mediated ferroptosis. / 美国人参来源的细胞外囊泡样纳米颗粒（AGELn）通过抑制GPX4介导的铁凋亡来减轻多柔比星诱导的心脏毒性。；Apigenin-5-O-glucoside alleviates hepatic fibrosis through NOX4-mediated ferroptosis in zebrafish. / 着脂素-5-O-葡萄糖苷通过NOX4介导的铁凋亡缓解斑马鱼的肝纤维化。；Aspartame exposure impairs bone formation in zebrafish larvae through ferroptosis-associated redox dysregulation, accompanied by an NRF2-related antioxidant response / 阿斯巴甜暴露通过铁剥蚀相关的氧化还原失调，损害斑马鱼幼体的骨骼形成，伴随NRF2相关的抗氧化反应
- 焦亡：22 篇；CTSS/GSDMD Axis-Mediated Pyroptosis Mediates Tight Junction Disruption and Inflammatory Response Induced by Samarium Oxide in Zebrafish. / CTSS/GSDMD轴导的热减死介导斑马鱼中由氧化钔引起的紧密连接断裂和炎症反应。；Endoplasmic reticulum targeted photosensitizer with high photostability and dual ROS generation for breast cancer PDT via apoptosis and pyroptosis / 具有高光稳定性和双活性作用生成的乳腺癌PDT通过凋亡和热剥蚀产生的内质网光敏剂；Pyroptosis- and Necroptosis-Related Signaling in Salicylate UV Absorber-Induced Toxicity: Implications for Sustainable Chemistry and Human Health / 水杨酸紫外吸收剂引起的毒性中与热浸和死死害相关的信号传导：对可持续化学和人类健康的启示；Red-emitting carbon dots as highly sensitive fluorescent probes for detecting changes of ClO−in inflammatory systems and cell pyroptosis / 发红碳点作为高灵敏度荧光探针，用于检测炎症系统中ClO变化和细胞热剥蚀；Green tea epigallocatechin gallate attenuate metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease by regulation of pyroptosis / 绿茶表食子儿科异食子酸通过调控焦冻症减缓与脂肪肝相关的代谢功能障碍相关肝病
- 内质网应激：107 篇；3,5-Dibromo-4-hydroxybenzaldehyde Induced neurodevelopmental defects via endoplasmic reticulum stress-mediated apoptotic pathway in Zebrafish. / 3,5-二溴-4-羟基苯甲醛通过内质网应激介导的凋亡途径诱导斑马鱼的神经发育缺陷。；Correction: Synergistic modulation of endoplasmic reticulum stress pathway, oxidative DNA damage and apoptosis by β-amyrin and metformin in mitigating hyperglycemia-induced renal damage using adult zebrafish model / 更正：利用β-卵蜜和二甲双胍协同调节内质网应激通路、氧化DNA损伤和凋亡，以减轻高血糖诱发的肾损伤，利用成年斑马鱼模型；Developmental and systemic toxicity of the triazole fungicide tetraconazole in zebrafish: Integrated evidence from ER stress, apoptosis, and metabolomic disruption. / 三唑杀菌剂四康唑在斑马鱼中的发育和全身毒性：来自内质网应激、凋亡和代谢组破坏的综合证据。；Early-Life Exposure to InP/ZnS QDs Triggers Photoreceptor Apoptosis via ER Stress in Zebrafish: Implications for Nanomaterial-Induced Retinal Toxicity. / 早期暴露于InP/ZnS QDs通过内质网应激引发斑马鱼的光感受器凋亡：对纳米材料诱导视网毒性的启示。；ERα-mediated endoplasmic reticulum stress drives 4-tert-octylphenol-induced cardiac developmental toxicity in zebrafish. / ERα介导的内质网应激驱动斑马鱼中4-三甲辛酚诱导的心脏发育毒性。
- 线粒体应激：3 篇；Acute hypoxia induces transient olfactory dysfunction through olfactory epithelial degeneration and bulbar mitochondrial stress in zebrafish. / 急性缺氧通过嗅觉上皮退化和斑马鱼球粒体应激引发暂时性嗅觉功能障碍。；Geraniol and Z-guggulsterone co-treatment attenuates Alzheimer’s disease in adult zebrafish by targeting oxidative damage, neuroinflammation, mitochondrial stress, and synaptic dysfunction / Geraniol和Z-古格尔酮的联合治疗通过针对氧化损伤、神经炎症、线粒体应激和突触功能障碍，减缓成年斑马鱼的阿尔茨海默病；The pesticide dieldrin disrupts proteins related to oxidative respiration and mitochondrial stress in the central nervous system / 农药二耳酮破坏中枢神经系统中与氧化呼吸和线粒体应激相关的蛋白质
- 蛋白质稳态：14 篇；Impaired Proteostasis is Linked to Neurological Pathology in a Zebrafish NGLY1 Deficiency Model. / 蛋白质平衡受损与斑马鱼NGLY1缺乏模型中的神经病理有关。；Intersection between proteostasis disturbances and time-of-day vulnerability to neuronal and visual dysfunction in a zebrafish FASD model / 斑马鱼FASD模型中蛋白质稳态障碍与神经元和视觉功能障碍易感性交织；Manipulation of Proteostasis Networks in Transgenic ZAAT Zebrafish via CRISPR-Cas9 Gene Editing. / 通过CRISPR-Cas9基因编辑操作转基因ZAAT斑马鱼的蛋白质稳态网络。；Dysregulated TDP-43 proteostasis perturbs excitability of spinal motor neurons during brainstem-mediated fictive locomotion in zebrafish. / TDP-43蛋白稳态失调在斑马鱼脑干介导的假动作中扰乱脊髓运动神经元的兴奋性。；Proteostasis governs differential temperature sensitivity across embryonic cell types. / 蛋白质稳态控制胚胎细胞类型间的温度敏感性差异。

### 纤毛、细胞器与膜运输

- 纤毛：292 篇；⍺TAT1-dependent microtubule acetylation is required for touch sensation in zebrafish but not for cilia-driven morphogenesis. / ⍺TAT1依赖的微管乙酰化是斑马鱼触觉感知的必需，但纤毛驱动的形态发生则不需要。；Correction: Tulp3 deficiency results in ciliopathy phenotypes during zebrafish embryogenesis / 更正：Tulp3缺乏导致斑马鱼胚胎发育期间的纤毛病表型；Insights into the zebrafish Left-Right Organizer's Centrosomes and Cilia via Volume Electron Microscopy. / 通过体积电子显微镜对斑马鱼左右组织者的中心体和纤毛的洞察。；mRNA therapy improves the composition and motility in CCDC40-deficient cilia in vitro and in vivo. / mRNA疗法在体外和体内改善CCDC40缺乏纤毛的组成和运动能力。；Synthesis and biological evaluation of heteroarylnitrile derivatives for motile ciliogenesis / 杂腈衍生物的合成与生物学评估，用于运动性纤毛生成
- 线粒体功能：139 篇；Arsenite methyltransferase 3 is required for mitochondrial function and hepatic lipid metabolism. / 砷酸甲基转移酶3是线粒体功能和肝脂质代谢所必需的。；Bis(2-ethylhexyl)-2,3,4,5-tetrabromophthalate Induces Neurodevelopmental Toxicity through the Gadd45b-MKK7-p21 Pathway: Cell Cycle Arrest and Mitochondrial Dysfunction. / Bis（2-乙基己基）-2,3,4,5-四溴邻二甲酸酯通过Gadd45b-MKK7-p21途径诱导神经发育毒性：细胞周期停止和线粒体功能障碍。；CuO Nanoparticles Trigger Cuproptosis-Linked Mitochondrial Damage and Gut Microbiota-Metabolome Disruption in Zebrafish. / CuO纳米颗粒会触发斑马鱼中与蜂巢管菌相关线粒体损伤和肠道微生物群-代谢组的破坏。；Deficiency of pnkp in zebrafish causes microcephaly, seizures, and developmental delay through mitochondrial dysfunction. / 斑马鱼缺乏PNKP会导致小头畸形、癫痫发作以及线粒体功能障碍导致发育迟缓。；Glabridin promotes melanosome degradation and alleviates melanosome-induced mitochondrial dysfunction in keratinocytes via autophagy / 格拉布里丁通过自噬促进黑色素体降解，并缓解角质细胞中黑色素体诱导的线粒体功能障碍
- 溶酶体：144 篇；A novel tetrahydrocarbazole derivative-based lysosome-targeted fluorescent probe for the detection of apoptosis and polarity change in biological system. / 一种新型的四氢卡巴唑衍生物基溶酶体靶向荧光探针，用于检测细胞凋亡和生物系统极性变化。；A phenazine-based turn-on fluorescent probe with preferential lysosomal localization for hypochlorous acid detection in living cells and zebrafish / 一种基于苯乙胺的开启荧光探针，具有优先定位的溶酶体定位，用于活细胞和斑马鱼的次氯酸检测；Aconitine outperforms mesaconitine and hypaconitine in triggering excessive mitophagy via lysosomal two-pore channels disruption in SH-SY5Y cells and zebrafish. / 乌头碱在通过溶酶体双孔通道破坏触发SH-SY5Y细胞和斑马鱼中过度线粒体吞噬方面优于米萨康丁和高波康尼丁。；Design and synthesis of a methylation-modified bifunctional fluorescent probe with Tunable ACQ–AIE transition for ultrasensitive detection of trace water and polarity in lipid droplets and lysosomes / 设计并合成一种带有可调ACQ–AIE转变的甲基化修饰双功能荧光探针，用于超灵敏检测脂滴和溶酶体中的微量水和极性；Enhanced lysosomal exocytosis and altered growth factor signaling are associated with cartilage pathology in a zebrafish model of MPSIVA. / 斑马鱼模型中的溶酶体胞吐作用增强和生长因子信号传导改变与软骨病理有关。
